# Changelog of the data structures

Changes to the tables, schemas and scripts (not to individual claims). Newest first.

## 9 November 2026 — Triage count correction, roadmap 372 (branch `midwar-ends`, commit 5)

- **Roadmap 372 split:** old value "22 mid-war, 67 war's end, 67 unknown", new value "23 mid-war, 67 war's end, 66 unknown". Reason: of the two units reclassified in chunk 2, only the 277th VGD came from mid-war; the 344th Infantry Division was in the unknown list (24-1=23 mid-war, 65+2=67 war's end, 67-1=66 unknown).
- **344th Infantry Division wording:** roadmap 372 old value "moved the 277th VGD and the 344th Infantry Division from mid-war to war's end", new value "moved the 277th VGD from mid-war to war's end and the 344th Infantry Division from unknown to war's end"; the chunk 2 entry below now names where each unit moved from. Reason: the 344th was triaged as unknown in commit 1. Docs only; no data changed.

## 9 November 2026 — Roadmap 370, 371 and 357 review (branch `midwar-ends`, commit 4)

- **Roadmap 370 (c), 159th Infantry Division:** new redesignation rows `C8973` (9 October 1944, Lexikon infantry division page `R613`, preferred) and `C8974` (29 September 1944, reserve division page `R871`, alternate) in conflict group `G117`, at the October 1944 table place (Colmar, 50 km, `Location caveat`), 0.3.
- **Roadmap 370 (d), 462nd Infantry Division:** `C8935` conflict_group_id old value blank, new value `G116` (preferred); map rows `C4078`, `C4240`, `C4371` claim_status old value preferred, new value alternate, conflict_group_id old value blank, new value `G116`, reason: the 462nd was redesignated a Volksgrenadier division by the beginning of November 1944 (lineage `UL007`), so the old label on the map is stale.
- **Coordinate exceptions:** 3 conflict-distance rows for `G116`.
- **Roadmap 371:** the 7th Armored Division ETO-OB table re-read (archived copy); `CR0409` and `CR0156` confirmed (in force on 8 May 1945). No relation changed; the remaining items are documented with their reasons in roadmap 371.
- **Roadmap 357:** each of the 83 pairs re-classified: 16 closed (text row is an objective or approach route), 10 closed (text row is a command post of another date: a sequence), 1 resolved earlier (`G113`), 56 still open with the reason (41 a day apart, 15 on the same day). No data changed.
- **Roadmap 370 (a) and (b)** stay open with the reason in the roadmap.

## 9 November 2026 — Mid-war German ends, chunk 2: Panzer-Brigades 108 and 111 (branch `midwar-ends`, commit 3)

- **End rows:** `C8971` Panzer-Brigade 111 disbanded and absorbed by the 11th Panzer Division with effect from 1 October 1944 (Lexikon, new reference `R880`, and Wikipedia `R701`: two families, approximate place Arracourt, 0.5); `C8972` Panzer-Brigade 108 disbanded with effect from 30 October 1944 after being pulled out north-east of Aachen-Geilenkirchen (Lexikon, new reference `R881`, 0.3; the unit record's Wikipedia note says only "November 1944").
- **Conflict groups:** `G114` (111): the end row is preferred; the 12th Army Group map rows `C3260`, `C3360`, `C3536`, `C3670`, `C3816` (3-31 October 1944) are alternates. `G115` (108): the end row is preferred; map rows `C3757` (31 October) and `C4046` (14 November) are alternates. Each edited row gives claim_status and conflict_group_id old value, new value and reason (stale map symbol after the disbandment).
- **Coordinate exceptions:** 5 conflict-distance rows for `G114` and `G115` (stale weekly map positions).
- **Reclassified in the triage (roadmap 372):** the 277th VGD (its staff was captured on 9 March 1945, but remnants fought on to the Harz in April) and the 344th Infantry Division (the first 344th was disbanded at Linfort on 14 October 1944 and the 91st Infantry Division then forming was renamed 344th, which went to the Eastern Front at the end of December 1944 and ended at Berlin) are war's-end units, not mid-war conflicts. The 277th VGD moved from mid-war and the 344th Infantry Division from unknown (corrected in commit 5).

## 9 November 2026 — Mid-war German ends, chunk 1: ten units (branch `midwar-ends`, commit 2)

- **End rows (10, Lexikon der Wehrmacht after Tessin, one family, 0.3):** `C8961` LXXXIX Corps, staff captured 29 March 1945 after encirclement in the Taunus (point Idstein, new place `PL4009`, 25 km); `C8962` 19th VGD officially disbanded 26 March 1945 (Kaiserslautern area, 25 km); `C8963` 77th Infantry Division lost with the fall of Saint-Malo, 15 August 1944 (destroyed; the disbandment order of 15 September 1944 is in the notes); `C8964` 91st Luftlande Division disbanded 10 August 1944 (last recorded place Subligny, 50 km); `C8965` 17th Luftwaffe Field Division scattered between Ghent and Sint-Niklaas and disbanded 28 September 1944 (point Lokeren, new place `PL4011`); `C8966` Panzer-Brigade 150 disbanded 25 December 1944 (last recorded place Malmedy); `C8967` 272nd VGD, destroyed by the end of March, officially disbanded 12 April 1945 (last recorded place, Remagen front, 50 km); `C8968` Korps Feldt disbanded 19 December 1944 by order of the First Parachute Army (Geldern sector); `C8969` 79th VGD scattered between Idar-Oberstein and Baumholder 18 March 1945 and `C8970` disbanded after crossing the Rhine, 24 March 1945 (Palatinate Forest point, new place `PL4012`, 50 km). Rows whose source gives no place carry a `Location caveat` sentence naming the point used.
- **Edit:** `C8955` (Panzer-Brigade 107 at Baumholder, 9 November 1944) `disestablished` old value false, new value true, reason: the brigade was dissolved on 7 November 1944 into the 25th Panzergrenadier Division (R879); a lineage row is not possible (cycle with `UL008`).
- **Tests:** scenario 115 now reads only the 79th VGD rows before `C8961`; new scenario 207.
- **Place:** a duplicate Kaiserslautern place created by the geocoder was removed before commit; `C8962` uses the existing `PL3864` (id `PL4010` is skipped).

## 9 November 2026 — Mid-war German ends, step 1: priority rule and triage of roadmap 372 (branch `midwar-ends`, commit 1)

- **New standing rule (Scott, 9 November 2026):** end rows for German units that ended at the war's end are low priority (future work); mid-war ends still matter. Recorded in HANDOFF rule 20, `docs/methodology.md` (last bullet) and roadmap 372.
- **Triage of roadmap 372 (156 units):** 24 mid-war formations, 65 war's end (60 formations, 5 battle groups), 67 unknown (31 formations, 36 battalions and battle groups); the lists are in roadmap 372. No data changed.

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 4: weak map-against-text pairs (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 6)

- **Map rows capped (62):** every 12th Army Group situation-map row (`R781`) in the pairs of roadmap 357 and 358 that stood above 0.3 (0.5 or 0.7) is now 0.3, so no map row outranks its text row; each row's notes give the old value, the new value and the reason. No row was deleted.
- **Conflict group `G113`:** 75th Infantry Division, text row `C0595` (Colmar, 31 January 1945) preferred and map row `C5492` (Rogery, 30 January) alternate, because Clarke and Smith, Riviera to the Rhine p. 549, say the division began moving into the First French Army area on 27 January.
- **New text row `C8960`:** 3rd Armored Division clears Dessau on 23 April 1945 (MacDonald, The Last Offensive p. 400: the attack began on 21 April, two days of house-to-house fighting, and one more day against a hold-out on the railroad to Rosslau; 0.5). It agrees with map row `C6809` (24 April, Kochstedt). The Wikipedia row `C1865` (corps reserve at Sangerhausen, 24 April) is kept with a context note; the pair stays open because the CMH text does not date the move.
- **Context notes:** `C2658` (1st Infantry Division near Mayenne on 8 August 1944, supported by Blumenson, Breakout and Pursuit p. 445).
- **Roadmap 358 decided:** the four headquarters-against-front pairs are kept as rows that may both hold (a corps or division symbol and a front or headquarters point 28-40 km apart); the two Canadian second text rows already sit against map rows that are alternates in `G082` and `G083`. Map rows of these pairs are capped as above.
- **Left open:** roadmap 357 (updated): the other pairs have no better dated text in the six CMH volumes read.

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 3, chunk C: Elbe and Enns surrenders (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 5)

- **End rows (4):** `C8956` Twelfth Army, bulk across the Elbe at Tangermünde and Fischbeck into American captivity by 7 May 1945 (Lexikon and MacDonald, The Last Offensive p. 464: two families, 0.5); `C8957` XXXIX Panzer Corps gathers at Fischbeck (new place `PL4008`) and begins to cross the Elbe, 4 May 1945 (Lexikon, 0.3); `C8958` 12th SS Panzer Division and `C8959` 9th SS Panzer Division cross the Enns demarcation line into American captivity, 8 May 1945 (Lexikon, 0.3, `eastern_front`; the town of Enns is a representative point, precision 50 km, because only the river is named).
- **Read and left open (roadmap 372 updated):** all remaining Lexikon pages of the audit list were scanned for a dated end with a place; the further units found have a date without a place or a place without a date (listed in roadmap 372).

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 3, chunk B: more German end rows (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 4)

- **End rows (2):** `C8952` 15th Panzergrenadier Division, British captivity at Lamstedt (new place `PL4006`) with the OB Nordwest capitulation, 5 May 1945 (Lexikon `R609`, 0.3); `C8953` 416th Infantry Division, remnants broken up by American units at Wasserburg am Inn (new place `PL4007`), 1 May 1945 (Lexikon, 0.3).
- **Panzer brigades (new references `R878`, `R879`, the Lexikon pages of Panzer-Brigade 105 and 107):** lineage `UL019` Panzer-Brigade 105 absorbed into the 9th Panzer Division, October 1944 (month only), with row `C8954` (withdrawn from the line at Stolberg and refilled at the end of September 1944, under the 9th Panzer Division, 0.3; relation `CR1281`). Panzer-Brigade 107: row `C8955` (arrival by rail at Baumholder on 9 November 1944, used to re-form the 25th Panzergrenadier Division, 0.3); no lineage row, because `UL008` already links the 25th Panzergrenadier Division to the brigade and the validator does not allow a lineage cycle; the end is in `unit_note`.
- **Audit rule (scratch tool, not in the repository):** a `YYYY-MM` lineage date now covers the whole month, so rows within the month are not "after" it.
- **Left open:** roadmap 372 (updated).

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 3, chunk A: end rows for German units whose claims stop (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 3)

- **Why:** fix 3 of the four consistency fixes Scott approved on 9 November 2026. Audit on the branch after fix 2: 173 German units whose last preferred row is not an end (`surrender`, `destruction`, `redesignation`, a `destroyed` or `disestablished` flag) and that are not a lineage predecessor; 132 of them are divisions, corps, armies or brigades, 98 with a Lexikon der Wehrmacht page in the references. The pages were read for a dated end with a named place. The 8 May rule is not used for German units. Nothing is deleted.
- **End rows (6, `C8946`-`C8951`):** `C8946` 462nd VGD, surrender of Fort Jeanne d'Arc (Feste Kaiserin) west of Metz `PL0224`, 13 December 1944, Lexikon and CMH The Lorraine Campaign p. 448, 0.7, `disestablished` true (the division was declared dissolved by order of 5 December 1944); `C8947` 26th VGD, last remnants surrender south of Blankenburg (Harz) `PL0649`, 21 April 1945; `C8948` 16th VGD, splinters surrender in the Kufstein area `PL0952`, 5 May 1945; `C8949` Sixth Panzer Army, remnants surrender to the Americans on the Enns near Steyr (new place `PL4003`), 8 May 1945 (the source gives 8/9 May), theater `eastern_front`; `C8950` 340th VGD, `destruction` after the loss of Rottbitze (new place `PL4004`), 17 March 1945; `C8951` 212th VGD, `destruction` in the Forstenrieder Park south of Munich (new place `PL4005`), 28 April 1945. All Lexikon only at 0.3 except `C8946`.
- **Lineage rows (4, `absorbed_into`, Lexikon):** `UL015` 49th Infantry Division into the 246th VGD, 5 December 1944 (order); `UL016` 48th Infantry Division into the 559th VGD, mid-November 1944 (entered 15 November); `UL017` 16th Luftwaffe Field Division into the 21st Panzer Division, 4 August 1944 (its infantry; the rest went to the forming 16th Infantry Division); `UL018` 18th VGD into the 26th VGD battle group, mid-March 1945 (entered 15 March). Both unit records name the linked record in `unit_note`.
- **Not entered (source conflict):** 344th Infantry Division (Lexikon: disbanded at Linfort on 14 October 1944; CMH `C0967` and the situation maps keep the division in the line to 19 December 1944); 277th VGD (Lexikon: staff captured at Niederlützingen on 9 March 1945, but CMH `C1170` has contingents opposite Remagen on 17 March and the Lexikon has small parts escaping across the Rhine). Both go to roadmap 372.

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 2: stale and parentless command relations (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 2)

- **Why:** fix 2 of the four consistency fixes Scott approved on 9 November 2026 (roadmap 352-354). Re-derived on the branch after fix 1: 27 open relations with claims more than 90 days after their last listing, 51 units with claims but no relation (17 with an organic relation only), and units smaller than a regiment without a traceable parent chain. Nothing is deleted; each changed relation carries old value, new value and reason in `note`, each changed claim in `notes`.
- **Stale German relations closed from the Lexikon der Wehrmacht unit tables (12, `valid_to_basis` `assignment_table`, `end_unknown` false):** `CR0623` (559th VGD under XIII SS Corps, to 30 November 1944), `CR0696` (277th VGD under I SS Panzer Corps, to January 1945), `CR0700` (62nd VGD under LXVI Corps, to February 1945), `CR0712` (36th VGD under XIII SS Corps, to January 1945), `CR0750` (719th Division under the 15th Army, to mid-January 1945), `CR0782` (189th Division under LXIV Corps, to February 1945), `CR1220` (XXV Corps under the 7th Army, to August 1944: Festung Lorient under the OKW), `CR1223` (47th Division under LXXXII Corps, to August 1944), `CR1233` (348th Division under LXVII Corps, to mid-August 1944), `CR1236` (19th Luftwaffe Field Division under LXXXIX Corps, to May 1944), `CR1238` (157th Reserve Division under LXIV Reserve Corps, to April 1944), `CR1241` (165th Reserve Division under LXXXIX Corps, to the renaming as the 70th Division on 1 August 1944, `UL010`). `valid_to_earliest` is the last Lexikon month that still lists the corps, `valid_to` the last day before the month that lists the next one. The last linked claim of each relation now carries the end as `stated_effective_to` (basis `assignment_table`, role `asserts_end`) with a note that the claim itself does not date the end and the Lexikon does (same convention as `CR0447`). The Lexikon pages were already references; the XIII SS Corps page (note on `CR0603` only) is cited by URL.
- **Stale relations confirmed and kept open (7 with `CR0888` below, note only):** `CR0603`, `CR0697`, `CR0716`, `CR0735`, `CR1204` (the Lexikon lists the same higher unit through the last claim) and `CR0713` (17th SS PGD: the Lexikon has "z. Vfg. 1. Armee" for January 1945, but CMH has the division in the XIII SS Corps attack of January; not closed).
- **Allied relations:** `C7610` and `C7662` (49th Division, Stacey) parent blank -> `CAN-COR-001C`, linked to the existing attachment `CR0865` (and, as observed during it, to the standing `CR0888`, as `C1482` and `C1545` are); `CR0888` (49th Division assigned to I British Corps) kept open with a note: the Canadian corps relations are attachments and no source read ends the standing assignment. New placeholder unit `UK-DIST-NETH` (Netherlands District; Stacey pp. 585-586) with relations `CR1273` (33rd Armoured Brigade, `C7637`) and `CR1274` (4th Commando Brigade, `C7638`) from 13 April 1945; `CR0986`, `CR0987`, `CR1013` closed 1945-04-12 by the next-row rule (`CR0987` keeps `end_unknown` true: an attachment). 7th Armored Division: new row `C8940` (30 March 1945, dam at the east end of the Eder-See `PL2787`, capture, 0.5, CMH The Last Offensive) under III Corps (`CR1280`); note on `CR0409`.
- **Parent chains of units smaller than a regiment:** 3rd Parachute Division rows `C8404`, `C8405` (FMS B-541) and `C8213` (FMS B-240) parent blank -> `GER-FJ-002C`; `CR0008` `valid_from` old 1944-06-09 (claim_date), new 1944-01-15 (narrative, stated on the `C8404` link), so the division's battalions of 15 May 1944 have a chain. SS Heavy Panzer Battalion 101 `C8931` (13 June 1944) parent blank -> `GER-SSPZ-001C`; `CR1272` `valid_from` old 1944-07-15, new 1944-06-13.
- **Parentless divisions given a higher command (5 rows, 0.3, Lexikon month rows dated the 15th, sector of the named town):** `C8941` 462nd VGD under LXXXII Corps at Metz (November 1944, `CR1279`); `C8942` 256th VGD under LXXXIX Corps at Groningen (October 1944, `CR1278`); `C8943` 226th Division under LXVII Corps at Dunkirk (August 1944, `CR1275`); `C8944`, `C8945` 17th Luftwaffe Field Division at Le Havre (new place `PL4002`) under LXXXI Corps (May 1944, `CR1276`, closed 14 August by the next-row rule) and LXVII Corps (August 1944, `CR1277`).
- **Tests:** scenarios 126 and 127 ("new rows have blank parents") now exempt the four Stacey rows given a parent here; new scenario 204.
- **Left open:** roadmap 371.

## 9 November 2026 — Consistency fix 1: claim rows before formation or after the end of a unit (branch `wf-consistency-fixes`, commit 1)

- **Why:** Scott approved four consistency fixes on 9 November 2026; fix 1 is the lifespan audit (claims before `formed_date`, after an end row, or outside a lineage date; 48 rows in the audit of `main` before the theater pass). Re-derived on `main` 729bf99: 52 rows (the 48 plus 4 from the placeholder pass). Nothing is deleted; every changed row carries old value, new value and reason in its notes.
- **`formed_date` moved to the formation of the staff (10 units).** The old date was the date of the designation the record is named for, while rows before it describe the same staff under its earlier designation (Lexikon der Wehrmacht pages of the earlier designation, new references `R868`-`R877`). Old value -> new value: `GER-INF-176D` 1944-10 -> 1943-01-26 (Division Nr. 176); `GER-INF-180D` 1944-10-31 -> 1939-11-25 (Kdr. der Ersatztruppen 2/X, later Division Nr. 180); `GER-INF-189D` 1944-10-08 -> 1943-05-20 (189th Reserve Division); `GER-INF-159D` 1944-10-09 -> 1942-10-01 (159th Reserve Division); `GER-INF-190D` 1944-11-04 -> 1940-06-10 (Division Nr. 190); `GER-INF-462D` 1944-10-19 -> 1942-10-15 (Division Nr. 462); `GER-VG-553D` and `GER-VG-559D` 1944-10-09 -> 1944-07-11 (553rd and 559th Grenadier Divisions); `GER-PG-FUEBE` 1945-01-26 -> 1944-08; `GER-PG-FUEGR` 1945-01-26 -> 1944-07-10 (the brigades). `formed_date_source` moves to the earlier designation's page; `unit_note` gains a "Formed date" sentence with old value, new value and reason.
- **Redesignation rows (6, `C8934`-`C8939`, 0.3, Lexikon only)** so the record's own Lexikon page stays cited: 190th Infantry Division at Kleve (Reichswald) 1944-11-04; 462nd at Metz 1944-10-19; 553rd VGD at Nomeny (new place `PL4000`) 1944-10-09; 559th VGD near Château-Salins 1944-10-09; Führer-Begleit- and Führer-Grenadier-Division near Cottbus (new place `PL4001`, `home_germany`) 1945-01-26.
- **Kampfgruppe Chill:** `C7853` event_type other -> activation (FMS B-156: founded at Turnhout on 2 September 1944; `formed_date` 1944-09-04 from CMH kept, noted in `unit_note`).
- **Army Group H:** `C1037` event_type command_change -> activation (CMH 10 November 1944); `C7744` (FMS B-717, take-over at the end of October) preferred -> alternate; conflict group `G110`.
- **"destruction" rows of units that went on (5):** `C1777` (Panzer Brigade 112, Dompaire) -> attack; `C1182` (84th Division), `C7807` (712th), `C7478` (15th Panzer Grenadier), `C8174` (340th VGD, one battalion) -> defense. The `destroyed` flag was already false and later sourced rows follow each unit; `destruction` means the unit ended.
- **Stale map rows after a brigade ended (11 rows, 2 groups):** Panzer Brigade 113, disbanded 1 October 1944 (`C1782`, preferred), map rows `C3252`, `C3363`, `C3539`, `C3674`, `C3817` preferred -> alternate, group `G111`; Panzer Brigade 112, dissolved 23 September 1944 (`C1779`, preferred), map rows `C3181`, `C3265`, `C3374`, `C3554`, `C3684`, `C3820` preferred -> alternate, group `G112`.
- **462nd: 3 map rows annotated, not moved.** `C4078`, `C4240`, `C4371` (14-28 November 1944, map label "462 ZBV") are the same formation after its redesignation as the 462nd Volksgrenadier Division at the beginning of November 1944 (lineage `UL007`; CMH says "462d VG Division" on 9 November); a Context sentence says so. They stay on `GER-INF-462D` because a claim id never moves to another unit (`scripts/check_claim_ids.py`); roadmap 370 (d).
- **25th Panzergrenadier Division:** new row `C8933`, activation at Grafenwöhr 1944-09-20 (`home_germany`, 0.3, Lexikon `R282`): the re-formation that lineage `UL008` (remnants into Panzer Brigade 107) does not cover; `unit_note` updated.
- **No data change needed (re-formation or lineage kind):** `GER-FJ-003D` and `GER-COR-013C` (an activation row after the end row starts the second formation); `GER-INF-719D` and `GER-VG-016D` (lineage `absorbed_into` joins a successor that already existed). **Open:** `GER-INF-462D` `C4371` (above) and `GER-INF-JUTLAND` (second formation of 9 March 1945 has no place; `unit_note` updated; roadmap 370).
- **`data/coordinate_exceptions.csv`:** 20 `conflict` rows (old value none) explain the distances inside `G111` and `G112`: the alternates are weekly map positions, so they lie apart by design.
- **Tests:** `scripts/semantic_tests.py` scenario 203. **Docs:** methodology (`formed_date` and `destruction`), roadmap 370.

## 9 November 2026 — German placeholders, chunk B3: SS Heavy Panzer Battalion 101 (branch `placeholders-14-theater`, commit B3)

- **Why:** roadmap 365 (Scott, 9 November 2026): the battalion is kept only with a traceable parent chain (I SS Panzer Corps), otherwise dropped with a note. The chain is traceable: Ellis, Victory in the West vol. I (`R670`), p. 333, chart "Opposing forces in the British Sector—mid July", lists the 101 SS Heavy Tank Battalion in the Caen sector under I SS Panzer Corps (Panzer Group West), as does the existing claim `C0160` (composing units). `record_status` old value placeholder, new value active.
- **Claims: 2, `C8931`-`C8932`** (old value none; both `western_europe`): `C8931` Villers-Bocage (`PL0373`), 13 June 1944, attack, 0.5 (Ellis p. 254 for the place and day; Ellis does not name the Tiger unit, the identification is Wikipedia `R114`, as in the existing rows `C0096`, `C0097`); `C8932` Caen sector (`PL0057`, representative point, 25 km), 15 July 1944, 0.3, parent `GER-SSPZ-001C` (one family and a sector).
- **Relation:** `CR1272` SS Heavy Panzer Battalion 101 -> I SS Panzer Corps (assigned, from 1944-07-15; old value none).
- **Not entered:** the battalion's history after mid-July 1944 has no source read in this pass (roadmap 369).
- **Tests:** `scripts/semantic_tests.py` scenario 202. **Docs:** roadmap 365 closed.

## 9 November 2026 — German placeholders, chunk B2: XII and XIII Corps, Division Nr. 172 and 526, Jutland, 30th SS, Division z.b.V. 136 (branch `placeholders-14-theater`, commit B2)

- **Why:** as chunk B1 (Scott, 9 November 2026, roadmap 365; two families wherever possible, one family at 0.3 with the reason; rows of every front and period kept, each with its theater). Old value for every unit: `record_status` placeholder, no claim file; new value: active (set by `scripts/promote_placeholders.py`). Every new row says "old value none" in its review sentence.
- **Promoted: 7 units, 71 claims `C8860`-`C8930`** (49 `western_europe`, 18 `eastern_front`, 3 `home_germany`, 1 `scandinavia`): `GER-COR-013C` 27 (Lexikon `R255`, FMS B-052 `R829` and B-831 `R776`, CMH The Last Offensive; 3 families; 14 rows of 1939-1944 in Poland, Belgium, Russia and Ukraine, 13 rows of 1945 including the command posts of the B-831 annex); `GER-COR-012C` 10 (Lexikon `R256`, FMS B-831, CMH; 3 families; 8 Eastern Front rows 1940-1944, Vittel 1940, the acting XII Corps at Wiesbaden in March 1945); `GER-DIV-172` 2 (Lexikon `R867` new; one family, every row 0.3: Gniezno 1939, Mainz 1940; the formation at Wiesbaden on 26 August 1939 is before the dataset bound of 1 September 1939, so in the notes only); `GER-DIV-526` 8 (Lexikon `R177`, 12th Army Group maps `R781`, CMH The Lorraine Campaign; 3 families); `GER-INF-JUTLAND` 2 (Lexikon `R239`, Ellis vol. II `R671`; Esbjerg 1944 is `scandinavia`); `GER-SSGR-030D` 19 (CMH Riviera to the Rhine, FMS B-078 `R770`, 12th Army Group maps; 3 families; the nine map rows of 23 January-20 March 1945 carry a caveat that the unchanged map symbol may be stale); `GER-DIV-136ZBV` 3 (FMS B-156 `R811` with the editor's note in B-343 `R812`, maps; 2 families, all 0.3).
- **Relations: 4 new, `CR1268`-`CR1271`, 11 evidence links** (old value none): `CR1268` XII Corps -> Seventh Army (from 1945-03-22); `CR1269` XIII Corps -> Seventh Army (1945-02-20 to 1945-04-03, closed by the next claim; CMH puts the move to the First Army at about 26 March); `CR1270` XIII Corps -> First Army (from 1945-04-04); `CR1271` Jutland (325th) -> LXXXVI Corps (from 1945-04-10). The 1939-1944 rows carry no parent (the armies of the Eastern Front have no records; the Lexikon army is in the evidence).

## 9 November 2026 — German placeholders, chunk B1: Division Nr. 905 and 405, Hamburg, Potsdam, 249th and 703rd (branch `placeholders-14-theater`, commit B1)

- **Why:** Scott, 9 November 2026: "Go ahead and start the other 14 placeholders" (roadmap 365), with the rule of the promotion pass: two independent source families wherever possible, one family only at 0.3 with the reason in the notes; data of every front and period kept, each row with its theater. Old value for every unit: `record_status` placeholder, no claim file; new value: active (set by `scripts/promote_placeholders.py`). Every new row says "old value none" in its review sentence.

## 9 November 2026 — Theater per claim row; other-front rows (branch `placeholders-14-theater`, commit A)

- **Why:** Scott, 9 November 2026: "For the out of theatre data, I do want to keep those." Claim rows on other fronts were kept with the unit's theater (`western_europe`) and a note, because `theater` had to repeat `data/units.csv`; roadmap 362 proposed a per-claim theater and per-front operations.
- **Schema:** `schema/claims.schema.json` `repeatedColumns.fields` old value `unit_name, nationality, echelon, branch, theater`, new value `unit_name, nationality, echelon, branch` (reason: a row may be on another front than the unit's primary theater); the claim `theater` description now says the row's own theater and that it must equal its operation's.
- **Vocabulary `theater`:** new values `balkans` (Yugoslavia, Albania, Greece, Bulgaria, Aegean), `scandinavia` (Norway, Denmark), `home_germany` (the Reich of the time as a rear or home area: forming, training, refitting, replacement army). Descriptions: `western_europe` old value "from the Normandy landings ...", new value also the 1940 campaign, the 1940-1944 occupation and staging in Britain; `mediterranean` old value "Italy, Sicily, the Balkans and Greece, ...", new value "Italy, Sicily, Sardinia, Corsica ..." (the Balkans moved to `balkans`); `eastern_front` adds Slovakia and German territory reached by the Red Army; `other` drops "home-front units" (now `home_germany`). Vocabulary `country` gains HU and SK.
- **Operations:** 6 new holding umbrellas in `data/operations.csv` (date_basis `derived`, dates in the notes): `OP-FRONT-EASTERN` 1939-09-01 to 1945-05-11, `OP-FRONT-MEDITERRANEAN` 1940-06-10 to 1945-05-02, `OP-FRONT-BALKANS` 1940-10-28 to 1945-05-15, `OP-FRONT-SCANDINAVIA` 1940-04-09 to 1945-05-08, `OP-FRONT-NORTH-AFRICA` 1940-06-10 to 1943-05-13, `OP-HOME-GERMANY` 1939-09-01 to 1945-05-08.
- **Validator (`scripts/operations.py`):** new errors: a claim's `theater` differs from its operation's; an operation's theater differs from its parent's. The "no CMH campaign streamer covers the date" error now applies to `western_europe` rows only (old value: every row dated 6 June 1944-11 May 1945). The derived `campaign` no longer falls back to a streamer by date for operations outside `western_europe` (old value: any row inside a streamer's dates got that campaign).
- **`scripts/check_coordinates.py`:** bounding boxes for `balkans`, `scandinavia` and `home_germany`. Two speed exceptions (rail moves of the 167th Volksgrenadier Division and Volks-Artillerie-Korps 408) in `data/coordinate_exceptions.csv`.
- **Backfill (27 rows; rule in `docs/methodology.md`, "Theater per claim row"):** 10 to `eastern_front` / `OP-FRONT-EASTERN` (`C8773`-`C8778`, `C8781`-`C8784`), 9 to `home_germany` / `OP-HOME-GERMANY` (`C8772`, `C8627`, `C8670`, `C8677`, `C8686`, `C8717`, `C8651`, `C8807`, `C8748`), 1 to `scandinavia` / `OP-FRONT-SCANDINAVIA` (`C8731`), 7 kept `western_europe` (`C8660`-`C8666`); old values theater `western_europe` and operation `OP-PRE-INVASION` (or `OP-CAMP-RHINELAND` for `C8731`, `C8748`, `C8807`); the old note sentence replaced by a `Context:` sentence with old and new values. No other row changed.
- **New rows (roadmap 362):** 11 claims `C8816`-`C8826`, all 0.3 from the Lexikon (one family), 10 outside `western_europe`; places `PL3947`-`PL3955` (OpenStreetMap Nominatim, `R002`); `GER-VAK-406C` promoted from placeholder by `scripts/promote_placeholders.py` (first claim `C8822`).
- **Tests:** `scripts/semantic_tests.py` scenario 199 (theater values, no repeated theater, claim and operation theaters agree, the backfill and new rows, no streamer needed for an Eastern Front row, the theater-mismatch error, `build/all_claims.csv` carries the row theater).
- **Docs:** `schema/README.md` (claim and unit `theater`, operations `theater`, `repeatedColumns`, the theater vocabulary and a "Theater" paragraph), `docs/methodology.md`, `README.md`, roadmap 360 (update), 362 (closed), 367 (viewer default filter, proposal) and 368 (facts still without a row). `docs/json_fields.md`: a section on `theater` in `build/all_claims.json` and `build/claims.geojson` (a plain string per row, not a JSON cell; filter on it for the Western Front).
- **Viewer and grid:** no change. `WebUI` reads `build/all_claims.csv` and filters by nationality, echelon and operation, not theater, so it now also shows the other-theater rows; a default `western_europe` filter is proposed in roadmap 367.

## 9 November 2026 — Map against text: conflict groups (branch `wf-health-fixes`, commit 3 of 3)

- **Why:** the Western Front health audit (issue 6, `find_map_vs_text.csv`) found 142 pairs in which a preferred 12th Army Group situation-map row (`R781`) and a preferred text row of the same unit, a day or less apart, are more than 25 km apart with no conflict group. Rule approved by Scott on 9 November 2026: a dated text row is preferred over a map row that disagrees by more than 25 km within a day; the map row is kept as an alternate, nothing deleted.
- **Grouped: 32 conflict groups `G078`-`G109` covering 33 pairs.** Each text row keeps `claim_status` preferred and gets the group id; each map row goes from preferred to alternate (old value, new value and reason in its notes). Confidence is unchanged except `C2707` (map, 0.7 -> 0.5, the cap for an alternate row). Where the two rows differ in event type an `Event definition:` sentence was added. The groups: `G078` CAN-ARM-004D: text `C7346` preferred, map `C3389` alternate (47 km); `G079` CAN-ARM-005D: text `C7640` preferred, map `C6841` alternate (42 km); `G080` CAN-COR-002C: text `C7396` preferred, map `C3750` alternate (28 km); `G081` CAN-INF-002D: text `C7304` preferred, map `C3094` alternate (62 km); `G082` CAN-INF-003D: text `C7311` preferred, map `C2947` alternate (63 km); `G083` CAN-INF-003D: text `C7367` preferred, map `C3387` alternate (27 km); - **Not grouped, within both precisions (20 pairs):** the distance is less than the two precision radii added together, so the rows do not contradict each other; nothing changed.
- **Not grouped, different things (4 pairs) and second text rows (2 pairs) (roadmap 358):** a headquarters row against a front or main-body row less than 25 km beyond precision (both can hold), and two cases where one map row disagreed with two text rows on consecutive days (only the same-day text row was grouped).
- **Map row outranking the text row (map 0.5 against text 0.3):** 47 of the 142 pairs at this commit (the audit counted 44): 1 grouped, 33 in the weak-text list, 13 within precision. They are marked in roadmap 357.
- **Coordinate check:** the new groups add 22 `[conflict]` warnings (the two rows are farther apart than their radii); these disagreements are about place and the notes say so (methodology, Coordinates).

## 9 November 2026 — Army-group and army headquarters rows, Western Front (branch `wf-health-fixes`, commit 2 of 3)

- **Why:** the Western Front health audit (9 November 2026, issue 2) found long gaps in the army-group headquarters rows: 12th Army Group nothing after 20 December 1944, 6th Army Group 4 rows in all, 21st Army Group nothing from 22 November 1944 to 2 May 1945, Army Group B nothing from 12 December 1944 to 1 April 1945. Scott approved filling them from text sources on 9 November 2026 ("Yes, start the fixes in that order").
- **New claims `C8611`-`C8626` (16), all text-sourced headquarters rows** (`location_represents` headquarters; town centre, precision 5 km, `location_basis` `town_centroid` except `C8625`; each note gives old value none, new value the row, and the reason; parents left blank):
  - **6th Army Group (10):** Vittel 27 October and 7 November 1944 (de Lattre, `R686` pp. 206, 220); Vittel, Heritage Hotel, 24 November 1944 (Riviera to the Rhine ch. 24 p. 438, `R423`, 0.7); advanced command post at Phalsbourg from 14 December and on 22 December (`R686` pp. 299, 301); Vittel 27 December 1944, 5 and 11 January 1945 (`R686` pp. 302-303, 311-313, 323-324); Phalsbourg 18 and 27 March 1945 (`R686` pp. 413, 419; the 27 March row also The Last Offensive ch. 15, `R544`, 0.7).
  - **12th Army Group (1):** Luxembourg City 19 March 1945 (The Last Offensive ch. 13, `R542` p. 265, 0.7).
  - **21st Army Group (3):** Montgomery's command post at Zonhoven 28 November 1944 (Ellis vol. II, `R671` p. 165, 0.7) and at Hasselt 25 and 28 December 1944 (p. 190, 197; 0.5 and 0.7).
  - **Army Group B (1):** command post at Rimbach, 7 km north-west of Altenkirchen, about 10 March 1945 (FMS B-709 English, `R855` typed p. -15-, 0.3 for the approximate date).
  - **First Canadian Army (1):** headquarters at Grave on 12 April 1945 (Stacey, `R668` pp. 571-572, 0.5), in the audit's 2 April-4 May gap.
- **New places:** `PL3894` Vittel, `PL3895` Zonhoven, `PL3896` Rimbach (Oberirsen) (OpenStreetMap Nominatim, `R002`, searched 9 November 2026). Existing places reused: `PL0273` Phalsbourg, `PL0200` Luxembourg City, `PL0168` Hasselt, `PL0824` Grave.
- **Not filled (roadmap 355):** the sources already read give no headquarters place for 12th Army Group from 20 December 1944 to 19 March 1945 and after 19 March, for 21st Army Group from 28 December 1944 to 3 May 1945, for Army Group B from 12 December 1944 to 10 March 1945, Army Group G (December to March), Army Group H (November to January), the German First Army (January to February), the US Fifteenth Army and the Sixth Panzer Army after 23 January. Sixteen rows were added, not the 25-40 the audit estimated: no position was entered without a page that names it.
- **Checked against existing rows:** the US Seventh Army (late December, Saverne, Riviera p. 499) and the German Fifteenth Army (Biedenkopf, 29 March, Last Offensive p. 353) and Eleventh Army (Blankenburg, 23 April) already have rows within a few km and days; nothing added.

## 9 November 2026 — Command-chain repair, Western Front (branch `wf-health-fixes`, commit 1 of 3)

- **Why:** the Western Front health audit (9 November 2026) found standing relations left open long after their last listing, so `who_controls` answered "possible" for months where the sources already give the chain, and units with claims but no command relation. Scott approved the repair on 9 November 2026 ("Yes, start the fixes in that order").
- **Stale relations:** an open relation (`open_at_last_claim`) with preferred claims of its unit more than 90 days after its last listing, 6 June 1944 to 9 May 1945. Before: **64 relations, 672 claim-dates** (the audit gave 44 and 431 but did not record its method; this count is the one used here, `stale.py` in the private scratch). After: **20 relations, 56 claim-dates**, each a roadmap item (349, 352).
- **US divisions (CMH ETO-OB tables already cited, re-read through the Wayback Machine):** 12 divisions (4th, 26th, 28th, 29th, 35th, 36th, 76th, 79th, 87th, 90th and 102nd Infantry, 6th Armored). Standard table-end rule: a relation ends the day before the first row that no longer lists it (basis `assignment_table`); later runs are new relations (V-013: start at the first row of the run). Corps-column cells are `unspecified_subordination`; a cell marked "(Opns)"/"(Operations)" is `operational_control` and "(Adm)"/"(Admin)" `administrative_control` (my reading of the qualifier, flagged for Scott). 100 new relations (`CR1084` onward). 95 new claims (`C8508`-`C8602`), one `subordinate_assignment` row per table row after the last listing of the earlier chain, at the command post the same page lists (precision 10 km, confidence 0.5; 0.3 and 25 km where the command post in force could not be placed). 48 new places `PL3846`-`PL3893` (OpenStreetMap Nominatim, R002), existing places reused where the town already exists (`PL2391` Oermingen, `PL3341` Sibret, `PL2668` Mendig for Niedermendig). Where a closed relation had no new claim in its run, the closing date is stated on its latest existing ETO-OB link (CR0155 on C0277, CR0246 on C0547, CR0259 on C0411, CR0282 on C0435, CR0434 on C0605).
- **III and VIII Corps:** `CR0179` (III Corps -> Third Army) closed on 11 February 1945, earliest 10 February (basis `inferred`: Bradley's order of 10 February, The Last Offensive p. 96, R657; First Army tables from 12 February); new III Corps -> First Army 11 February (latest 12 February) to 18 April (table end, earliest 4 April) and III Corps -> Third Army from 19 April (99th, 86th Infantry and 14th Armored Division tables; p. 424). `CR0189` (VIII Corps -> Third Army) closed on 21 April 1945 (6th Armored Division table); new VIII Corps -> First Army 22 April to 10 May (earliest 5 May) and VIII Corps -> Ninth Army from 6 May (latest 11 May). Minimal evidence claims `C8603`-`C8609` (as `C1278`), at the nearest 12th Army Group map position of the corps headquarters (0.3, 25 km; `C8607` 0.5 on the same-day map point). Two overlap rows: `CR0179`/`CR1184` (11 February) and `CR1186`/`CR1187` (6-10 May, the two tables differ).
- **German relations closed from claims already in the data:** `CR0736` LXXXVIII Corps -> Twenty-fifth Army closed 3 April 1945 (narrative, B-762 p. 2, `C7836`; `C7830` linked) and a new relation back to the army from 6 April (`C7838`); `CR0682` 15th Panzer Grenadier -> XLVII Panzer Corps closed 26 February (inferred, `C7452` linked), new LXIII Corps 27 February-4 March (`C7756`, `C7777`) and Army Group H reserve from 5 March (`C8002`); `CR0677` 47th Volksgrenadier -> LXXXI Corps closed 26 November 1944, new LXXIV Corps 27 November-8 December (B-811, `C8044`, `C8060`), LXXXI Corps 9 December-2 February (`C7062`), LXXXIX Corps 3-19 February (`C7063`), XC Corps from 20 February (`C7065`, `C7067`); `CR0714` 11th Panzer reserve closed 22 February 1945; `CR1043` Panzer-Brigade 106 -> Nineteenth Army closed 31 January 1945 with the LXIV Corps relation (inferred; `C1765` linked); `CR0775` XXXIX Panzer Corps -> Army Group Oberrhein closed 29 January 1945, the day the army group was dissolved (new minimal claim `C8610`, Riviera p. 548 and Lexikon); `CR0014` 711th Division -> Fifteenth Army closed 14 July 1944 and `CR0372` 6th Parachute Regiment -> 91st Division closed 5 September 1944 (inferred from the next row).
- **UK placeholders:** the `unknown` relations `CR0058`, `CR0375`, `CR0065` and `CR0079` closed the day before the named standing relation that follows (inferred from the next row, as `CR0119`).
- **Units with claims but no relation:** before, 69 units (301 claim-dates in the window) had no relation other than an organic one. 25 relations added from the units' own rows where a source names the higher unit (183rd and 363rd Volksgrenadier, 190th, 361st and 471st Infantry, 16th Luftwaffe Field, Parachute Division Erdmann, 34th SS, Division 406, 331st Infantry (reserve), German XXX Corps, Korps Ems, Wehrkreis XI, Kampfgruppen Walther and Katzmann, Polizei-Regiment Mönchengladbach, French 1st Infantry Division, 1st Canadian Armoured Brigade). After: 51 units (169 claim-dates), 13 of them regiments, battalions or battle groups whose only relation is the organic one to their division (a traced chain); the battle groups and independent regiments left without a parent are allowed (Scott's rule); the divisions and other formations are roadmap 354.
- **Relations changed (old value -> new value):** CR0014 (GER-INF-711D -> GER-ARMY-015A, assigned): valid_to blank (open_at_last_claim) -> 1944-07-14, earliest 1944-06-06 (inferred_from_next_claim); CR0058 (UK-ARM-004B -> unknown, unknown): valid_to blank (open_at_last_claim) -> 1944-06-26, earliest 1944-06-26 (inferred_from_next_claim); CR0065 (UK-ARM-GRDD -> unknown, unknown): valid_to blank (open_at_last_claim) -> 1944-07-17, earliest 1944-06-28 (inferred_from_next_claim); CR0079 (UK-INF-053D -> unknown, unknown): valid_to blank (open_at_last_claim) -> 1944-07-14, earliest 1944-06-27 (inferred_from_next_claim); ## stacey-victory-campaign: Stacey, The Victory Campaign, remaining maps and sketches; map-row conflict sweep (3 November 2026)

Eleventh commit of the approved Stacey pass (`R668`). Viewed the remaining maps and sketches: Map 9 (Ardennes crisis), Sketch 34 (threat across the Maas, mid-December 1944), Sketch 35 (Kapelsche Veer), Sketch 37 (Moyland Wood), Map 10 (Veritable), Sketch 38 (Hochwald Gap), Map 11 (Blockbuster), Sketches 40-44 (Rhineland, closing to the Rhine, Varsity, the Rhine left flank, Zutphen and Deventer), Map 12 (advance on the northern front), Map 13 (western Netherlands), Sketch 47 (advance across Germany), Sketch 49 (march to Wismar), Map 14 (final phase). Pages 474, 475, 510, 511, 545, 546, 656 and 657 are photographs and 702 is a table. Most map content repeats text rows already in the data; only facts with no row within a few days were added. Nothing deleted, no existing row changed.

- 11 new map rows `C7672`-`C7682` at 0.3 (5 German, 6 Allied; blank parents; places `PL3531`-`PL3538`): the 190th Infantry Division opposite Cuijk and Boxmeer (mid-December 1944); the 1st Canadian Parachute Battalion route with the 6th Airborne Division (Ladbergen, Minden, Ricklingen, Celle, Kolkhagen, Bahlendorf, 1-30 April); the 88th German Corps at Amersfoort and the 30th German Corps near Tiel (Map 13); Armeeabteilung Straube (First Parachute Army) and elements of Army Blumentritt (Map 14).
- Map-row conflict sweep over all Stacey rows `C7225`-`C7682` (same unit, within two days, farther apart than the precisions allow): 68 pairs, nearly all brigade-level rows against division points or movement over two days. Two new open points: roadmap 248 (4th Canadian Armoured, `C6663` map row against Stacey text rows at Sogel and Borger) and 249 (Armeeabteilung Straube map row against `C1238`). Scenario 128.

## confidence-corroborated: confidence raised where two independent source families agree (3 November 2026)

Approved by Scott on 3 November 2026.

- **Rule** documented in `docs/methodology.md` (confidence rubric, "Corroboration rule"): a row that gains an agreeing reference from a second `source_family` (geocoders excluded) is raised to 0.7; not for same-family pairs, conflict groups or rows named in an open map-conflict roadmap point.
- **Raised:** `C2181` (2d Panzer Division, Ortheuville, 20 Dec 1944) and `C2182` (116th Panzer Division, Samrée, 20 Dec 1944): confidence old value 0.5, new value 0.7, reason: FMS B-235 (`R772`) and Cole (`R388`, `R386`) agree.
- **Held, not changed:** `C1903` (28th Division, Neufchâteau; `R724` CMH Combat Chronicle and Cole are both `us_army_cmh`, one family); `C4787` (map row, roadmap point 233) and `C7136` (roadmap point 224), until Scott decides those map-conflict points.
- **Other rows with two or more families** (about 290 below 0.7) were given their confidence by other rules (inferred dates, representative points, CP-gap bands, compilation caps) and are not changed.
- **Semantic scenario 116.** Scenarios 44 (FMS B-235 rows at 0.5 or 0.3 only) and 114 (corroborated rows' confidence) updated to expect 0.7 on `C2181` and `C2182` (old expectation 0.5, new 0.7, reason: this rule).

## cole-ardennes: Cole, The Ardennes, second pass: Cole as a corroborating source on existing rows (3 November 2026)

- **Corroborations, no new mechanism.** The dataset has no separate corroboration table; a row with several sources lists every reference in `reference_ids`, every URL in `source` (joined by ` | `) and a locator per reference in `source_locator`. That existing multi-reference mechanism is used here. Five rows get a Cole chapter appended: `C4787` (101st Airborne, Mande-Saint-Étienne, 19 Dec; `R384` p. 307), `C1903` (28th Division, Neufchâteau, 22 Dec; `R385` p. 328), `C7136` (26th Volksgrenadier Division, Longvilly, 19 Dec; `R384` p. 304), `C2181` (2d Panzer Division, Ortheuville, 20 Dec; `R388` p. 429), `C2182` (116th Panzer Division, Samrée, 20 Dec; `R388` p. 429, `R386` p. 358). Each note records old and new `reference_ids` and the reason.
- **No value changed:** coordinates, dates, places and confidence are unchanged (`C4787` is a map row). Raising confidence where two source families now agree is left for Scott's review.
- **Semantic scenario 114.**

Scott approved reading NARA T-311 Army Group B rolls one commit per roll. Branch `nara-microfilm`. Roll 3 (NAID 271847205, 1378 images) read in the German original on 2 November 2026 with OCR triage; every claimed page image-checked. Reading notes private.

Rules applied: no command relation and no parent from a single source; strengths stay in notes; confidence 0.5 for dated documents naming a command post or place; disagreements logged without changing existing rows; Ic Dec Feindlage Allied picture kept in notes only.

### T-311 roll 3 (Ic Feindlagekarten Dec 1944; Ia Führer-Befehle, Tagesmeldungen Jun–Aug 1944, Op.Befehle Sep 1944)

- **New claims** `C7095` to `C7118` (24; all at 0.5; each note carries old value none, new value and reason): 346th Infantry Division command post at Putot-en-Auge (13 June); 84th Infantry Division assembling northwest of Falaise (1 August); 89th Infantry Division command post at Falaise (4 August); Panzer Group Eberbach at Vieux-Pont (13 August); 116th Panzer Division at Sées (12 August); 1st SS Panzer Division on the road at Rânes (13 August); Fifteenth Army command post at Dordrecht from 12 September; seventeen command posts from the 28 September 1944, 20.00 hrs Turnhout–Roermond Gliederung (LXVII Corps Breda; 346th Gooreind; 711th Minderhout; 719th Baarle-Nassau; LXXXVIII Corps Dongen; 245th Hilvarenbeek; 59th Boxtel; 85th/Kampfgruppe Chill St. Michielsgestel; XII SS Corps Driesprong; 9th SS Arnhem; 10th SS Pannerden; II Fallschirmjäger Corps Keppeln; 84th Wasserburg near Kleve; LXXXVI Corps Wankum; 180th Veulen; Fallschirmjäger Division Erdmann Belfeld; 176th Haus Effeld).

- **No relation, no conflict group** (next `CR1067`, `G034`).

- **Semantic scenario 98.** 47th Volksgrenadier Division Jan–Mar 1945 gap unchanged.

## nara-microfilm: NARA captured German records, T-315 roll 940 (47th Volksgrenadier Division; 2 November 2026)

Scott approved reading NARA captured-records microfilm as new sources on 2 November 2026, starting with T-315 roll 940 (NAID 346435955) and then the T-311 Army Group B rolls, one commit per roll (roll 4 done the same day). Branch `nara-microfilm` from `main` `e0d85d7`. The roll was downloaded from the public NARA catalog and read in the German original, all 142 frames (PDF pages 1-145) including the two hand-drawn maps; the reading notes stay private and are not in this repository.

Rules applied: no command relation and no parent from a single source (every new row has a blank parent); strengths stay in the reading notes; confidence 0.5 for a dated document naming the division sector, 0.3 where the sector is given only by a boundary; disagreements stay open points and no existing row is changed.

### T-311 roll 2 (Army Group B Ic Feindlagekarten, July-November 1944)

- **New reference** `R785` (archive_document, family `nara_captured_german_records`): NAID 271846540, 663 images, items 65881/8-/12. Read in the German original on 2 November 2026.

Per Scott (2 November 2026), German-perceived Allied positions from Ic maps stay in the private reading notes and are listed for a later decision; own-side corps stamps on the Eindhoven-Trier sheets were too coarse for dated place rows. No existing row changed; next claim id still `C7095`.
- **Semantic scenario:** none (zero claims).
- **47th Volksgrenadier Division Jan-Mar 1945 gap:** unchanged.

### T-311 roll 4 (Army Group B Ia operations orders and daily reports, 9 June-15 October 1944)

Reliability: 8 of 16 checkable facts agree within 15 km (rows of the same unit within 4 days).

The 190th Infantry Division row predates the unit formation date of 4 November 1944 (roadmap point 221).

- **Semantic scenario 97.** The roll has no 1945 material, so the 47th Volksgrenadier Division January-March 1945 gap is unchanged.

## loc-12ag-maps: HQ 12th Army Group situation maps, pass 1 (31 October 2026)

Scott approved a pass over the Library of Congress series *HQ 12th Army Group situation maps* (public domain, 416 daily sheets 6 June 1944 to 26 July 1945) on 31 October 2026: one sheet a week at the 1200 hrs situation, in batches, one commit per batch, on branch `loc-12ag-maps` from `main` `62e97c3`. The maps are a separate source family (contemporary Allied situation maps): Allied units from their own reports and German units from G-2 estimates.

Rules applied (accepted by Scott): one reference for the series with the sheet id and situation date in each claim's `source_locator`; division points read as the main body and corps or army symbols as the headquarters at a precision of 10 km; German units at confidence 0.3; Allied units at 0.5, or 0.7 when another source family has the unit within 10 km on the same or the next day; no command relation and no parent from the maps (every new row has a blank parent); no front-line trace; units shown en route, marked as elements, unlocated or doubtful, and brigades and regiments stay in the private reading notes; placeholder units get no first claim; a unit shown between documented dates gets a claim; a disagreement with an existing row is logged as an open question and the existing row is unchanged. Commander is `unknown` on every new row because the maps name none. Reading notes are private.

### Registration data in the repository (1 November 2026)

- `tools/loc12ag/sheetG_registration.json` checked against the working transforms (27 weekly sheets, `cs000106`-`cs000289`, all identical); new `tools/loc12ag/pixel_to_latlon.py` reproduces every map coordinate from the sheet pixel; README updated. No data rows changed.

### Roetgen place and name-merge guard (1 November 2026)

- **New place** `PL2611` Roetgen, North Rhine-Westphalia (GeoNames `R782`, PPLA4 id 2844277; old value none, new value this row, reason: central Roetgen readings were named "Rott" by reverse geocoding and merged by name into `PL2002` Rott, about 4 km away).

Notes added to each row.
- **Tool** `tools/loc12ag/name_merge.py`: prefer the municipality name within 1.5 km of a GeoNames seat; refuse a name merge into an existing place more than 3 km away. `PL2002` unchanged.

### Tools (commit 1)

- **New helper folder** `tools/loc12ag/` (not part of the build or the checks): `sheet_index.csv` (416 sheets by date, no missing day; 50 weekly sheets flagged for pass 1), `georef.py` (graticule fit through a Lambert conformal conic plane and an affine transform with residuals in km; registration of other scans of the same base sheet by phase correlation) and `sheet3_control.json` (base sheet 3, Normandy, 14 graticule ticks, RMS about 1 km). Needs numpy and Pillow.

### Batch 1: Normandy, 6 June-25 July 1944 (8 weekly sheets)

- **New reference** `R781` (the map series; archive_document, family `loc_12ag_situation_maps`).
- **Sheets read:** cs000001 (6 June), cs000008 (13 June), cs000015 (20 June), cs000022 (27 June), cs000029 (4 July), cs000036 (11 July), cs000043 (18 July), cs000050 (25 July), all at 1200 hrs.
- **New places** `PL1143` to `PL1186` (44; OpenStreetMap Nominatim): Normandy communes within 3 km of a map point where no existing place was within 3 km.
- **New claims** `C2282` to `C2468` (187: 108 German at 0.3, 69 British and 10 Canadian; 10 Allied rows at 0.7 and 69 at 0.5; each note carries old value none, new value and reason). Event type `other` for division points and `hq_observation` for corps and army headquarters; `location_basis` documented_on_date; each row keeps its own map point in `coord_override` within 3 km of its place.
  - Parent blank on all 187 (inferred value: old none, new blank, reason: no command relation is taken from the situation maps).
  - Commander `unknown` on all 187 (old none, new unknown, reason: the maps name no commander).
- **Agreement with the dataset:** 73 new rows have an existing row of the same unit within three days; 42 of them are within 10 km (58%). Most of the 31 others compare the map's main-body point with an objective or front-line point 10-20 km away; the larger differences are open points in `docs/roadmap.md`.
- **Not written:** seven readings of placeholder units (265th, 266th and 276th Infantry Divisions), three readings with no gazetteer place within 3 km, and every symbol marked elements, unlocated, doubtful or en route (private notes).
- **No existing row changed.** No relation, no conflict group (next `CR1067`, `G034`).
- **Semantic scenario 53.**

### Batch 2us: US units on the eight Normandy sheets, 6 June-25 July 1944

Scott on 31 October 2026 asked for US units on every sheet, not only gaps, to measure the maps against the dense US data and to give US units a second source family.

- **Rule added (accepted by Scott):** US rows at 0.5, or 0.7 when another source family has the unit within 10 km and within three days (old window for Allied rows: same or next day; reason: Scott's instruction for US units; British, Canadian and German rows keep the batch 1 rule). US division symbols are read as the main body at the box centre; corps, army and army group headquarters flags are read at the foot of the flag staff (the dot), where the sheet draws one. A division drawn twice (a boxed command post on a staff behind a box at the front, as on the 13 June sheet) is read only at the unstaffed box; the staffed division command posts are not written in this batch (private notes).
- **New claims** `C2469` to `C2582` (114 US: 92 division rows, 22 corps, army and army group headquarters; 28 at 0.7, 86 at 0.5). Parent blank on all (old none, new blank, reason: no command relation is taken from the maps); commander `unknown` on all (old none, new unknown, reason: the maps name no commander). First rows for the 12th Army Group headquarters on the maps (`US-AG-012G`, 25 July).
- **New places** `PL1187` to `PL1220` (34; OpenStreetMap Nominatim, communes within 3 km of a map point).
- **Agreement:** 45 new US rows have an existing row of another source family within three days; 28 are within 10 km (62%). Division rows 28 of 37 (76%); corps and army headquarters 0 of 8, all against objective or sector-centre rows 14-54 km away. Against existing headquarters or main-body rows only: 14 of 19 (74%).
- **Gaps:** 75 new rows have no existing row of the unit within two days, 49 none within six days.
- **Not written:** 9 readings with no gazetteer place within 3 km, 1 duplicate (35th Infantry Division, 11 July, same date and event type already present), the dashed XV Corps box of 18 July (en route).
- **`data/coordinate_exceptions.csv`:** one speed exception added, `C0214`/`C2528` (82nd Airborne Division, Normandy on 11 July, England on 13 July: a sea move after relief); old value none, new value the exception, reason: the 370 km in two days is the Channel crossing. `C2528` says so in its note; `C0214` is unchanged.
- **`scripts/semantic_tests.py`:** scenario 53 expectation of (echelon, location_represents) pairs, old value division, corps and army only, new value with `('army_group', 'headquarters')`, reason: batch 2us adds the 12th Army Group headquarters under R781. New scenario 54.
- **No existing row changed.** No relation, no conflict group.

- **Sheets read:** cs000057 (1 August, base sheet 3), cs000064 (8 August), cs000071 (15 August), cs000078 (22 August), all at 1200 hrs. The last three are new base sheets; each was fitted from 9 to 13 town junctions (`tools/loc12ag/sheetB_control.json` to `sheetD_control.json`, RMS about 1 km, leave-one-out at most 3.7 km).

Same rules as batches 1 and 2us; parent blank and commander unknown on all (old none, new blank or unknown, reasons as in batch 1).

Lower than in Normandy because units moved 30-200 km in three days during the breakout and pursuit (22 August against rows of 25 August on the Seine); see the roadmap.

- **New scenario 55.** No existing row changed. No relation, no conflict group.

### Village places for readings with no town within 3 km (batches 1, 2us and 3a)

Scott on 31 October 2026 approved adding village-level places for the 42 map readings that batches 1, 2us and 3a had skipped because no gazetteer town lay within 3 km.
- **New places** `PL1347` to `PL1384` (38; OpenStreetMap Nominatim hamlet and village points within 3 km of the map point, searched 31 October 2026). Names are `Hamlet (commune), Department` or `Village, Department`. Where OpenStreetMap gives a post-1944 merged commune, the 1944 commune is used: for villages that were 1944 communes (Sermentot, Étouvy, Coulonces, Saint-Martin-de-Tallevende, Champ-du-Boult, La Cochère, Le Roncenay-Authenay, Crennes) the note names the modern commune; for seven hamlets (`La Flague`, `La Marvindière`, `La Morlière de Bas`, `La Haute Barre`, `Les Moulins`, `Radray`, `La Saulnerie`) the 1944 commune is inferred from the nearest village centre and marked "to be confirmed"; two hamlets keep the modern commune (`Le Neufbourg (Le Molay-Littry)`, `La Sauvagère (Valdallière)`) and are listed for renaming in the roadmap. `La Fontaine Pesée (Étampes)` uses the 1944 department Seine-et-Oise (Essonne today).
- **New claims** `C2810` to `C2850` (41: 26 US, 5 British, 10 German; 7 at 0.7, 24 at 0.5, 10 at 0.3), written exactly as the other map rows (same confidence rules, representative point, precision 10 km, parent blank and commander unknown on all: old value none, new value blank or unknown, reason: no command relation or commander is taken from the situation maps). ### Place fix: `C0383` (9th Infantry Division command post, 8 August 1944)

Scott on 31 October 2026 approved re-pointing `C0383` (roadmap point 24).
- **`C0383`:** `place_id` old value `PL0355` (La Tuilerie, Calvados, 49.2190 N 0.7900 W, at Le Tronquay near Bayeux), new value `PL1385`; latitude and longitude old value 49.2190, -0.7900, new value 48.8299, -1.1282; `location_basis` old value `town_centroid`, new value `inferred`. Reason: the old point is about 45 km north of the division, behind the British sector. Evidence: CMH ETO-OB (`R372`) lists the command posts St-Aubin-des-Bois, Calvados (5 August), "La Tuilerie (1 mi SE)", Calvados (8 August) and Forien, Manche (9 August, Saint-Laurent-de-Cuves); the 12th Army Group map of 8 August (`C2651`, `R781`) puts the division near Saint-Michel-de-Montjoie, 10 km from the new point. The exact La Tuilerie was not found: no such name is in OpenStreetMap or in the IGN gazetteer (lieux-dits and addresses) in the 56 communes around Saint-Aubin-des-Bois, Saint-Michel-de-Montjoie and Saint-Laurent-de-Cuves (searched 31 October 2026). The new point is a representative point at Saint-Aubin-des-Bois, the last named command post and the only Calvados commune on the route, precision 10 km. Confidence unchanged (0.5).
- **New place `PL1385`** (La Tuilerie (not located), near Saint-Aubin-des-Bois, Calvados; OpenStreetMap Nominatim point of Saint-Aubin-des-Bois). `PL0355` is unchanged and still used by `C2621` (21st Army Group, 1 August, near Le Tronquay).
- **Scenario 56** now expects `C0383` on `PL1385` (old expectation: unchanged on `PL0355`).

### Rule of 31 October 2026: map rows are coarse filler

Scott on 31 October 2026 decided that the situation-map rows are coarse filler and never outrank a text row: from now on a map row is set to 0.7 (two source families) only when an existing non-map row of the unit agrees **within 5 km** (old value: within 10 km) on the same day (US: within three days). Applies to new rows from village pass 3 on; earlier rows are not changed. `tools/loc12ag` generation and the `R781` reliability note now say so (reliability_note old value "0.7 when another family agrees within 10 km on the same or the next day", new value adds the 5 km rule and its date). 54 of the 106 earlier map rows at 0.7 rest on agreement of 5-10 km (roadmap point 40, for Scott's decision).

### Village pass 5: GeoNames for the radius search (readings of 14 November 1944)

The OpenStreetMap Overpass service (main server and two mirrors) was unreachable from 31 October to 1 November 2026, so Scott on 1 November 2026 asked to switch the "named place within 3 km" search to an offline GeoNames gazetteer.
- **Method:** the free GeoNames country dumps NL, BE, LU, DE and FR (download.geonames.org/export/dump/, downloaded 1 November 2026, kept on the box only, never committed); populated places (feature class P: PPL, PPLL, PPLX, PPLF, PPLS, PPLA-PPLA4, PPLC, PPLG; historical and abandoned places excluded) searched locally within 3 km of the map point; nearest village preferred within 1 km of the nearest place. Nominatim (1.1 s between requests, retries with backoff) is used only to name the municipality and region. New reference **`R782`** (GeoNames, CC BY 4.0) is the coordinate reference of the places found this way.
- **New claims** `C4122` to `C4127` (6: 10th SS Panzer at Hoekelum, 51st Division at Boshoven, XXX Corps at Schootshei, 43rd Division at Heiwik, 361st at La Hoube, 16th at Hirtengaerten), written as the other rows of the sheet (0.3 German, 0.5 others; parent blank, commander unknown: old value none, new value blank or unknown, reason: no command relation or commander is taken from the situation maps).
- **New places** `PL2206` to `PL2211` (GeoNames points, local names).
- **Still unplaced:** the 85th Infantry Division (point on the water of the Haringvliet, no populated place within 3 km).

### Visual audit 5r–5t (1 November 2026)

Scott on 1 November 2026 asked to check every unchecked map reading by eye on its own sheet (roadmap points 199–200).
- **Method:** each row's coordinates projected back onto its sheet with the sheet registration, labelled marker tiles rendered, marker compared with the unit's symbol; 2–3 gazetteer towns projected per sheet (all within about 3 km of their printed names).
- **`cs000254` (13 February 1945):** 131 rows checked, 40 moved, 39 unresolved. **`cs000261` (20 February):** 134 checked, 39 moved, 52 unresolved. **`cs000268` (27 February):** 132 checked, 36 moved, 55 unresolved.
- **Moved rows:** old and new latitude/longitude, distance and old/new place are in each row's notes ("Visual audit 2 Nov 2026 (roadmap point 199)"); reason: the marker was more than 3 km from the labelled symbol of the unit. Place, location qualifier and coordinate override updated. New places `PL3161` to `PL3198` (GeoNames points, region from the nearest place of the same country).
- **Unresolved rows:** kept unchanged, note "unresolved, not moved: <reason>".
- **Tests:** `semantic_tests.py` accepts a new place on rows whose notes carry the audit annotation.

## fms-german-6: two more FMS manuscripts (B-745, B-151; 31 October 2026)

Scott approved reading B-745 on Fold3 and B-151 from the Internet Archive and one data branch for the two on 31 October 2026, one commit per manuscript. Branch `fms-german-6` from `main` `1082707`. B-745 is read in the German original. B-151 is read in the US Army English translation from the Internet Archive because neither B-151 nor its sequel B-151a is on the Fold3 M1035 fiche and the German original is held only at the Bundesarchiv; the item was catalogued as B-151a but every page is B-151. Both are US Government works; the claims paraphrase them in English. The reading notes stay private and are not in this repository.

Rules applied (accepted by Scott): no command relation and no parent from a single manuscript (every new row has a blank parent); no Allied dates from the manuscripts; strengths stay in the reading notes; no front-line traces; confidence 0.5 for one source, 0.7 when a second source family agrees, 0.3 for arrivals, approximate days, doubtful readings and map positions; disagreements with existing rows stay open points and no existing row is changed. No first claim is given to a placeholder unit (the 257th Volksgrenadier and 106th Infantry Divisions and Divisions Nr. 405 and 905 stay placeholders).

### B-745 (Brandenberger, Nineteenth Army, 1 April-5 May 1945, German original)

- **New reference** `R779` (B-745; archive_document, family `fms_german_manuscript`). Reliability: 8 of 12 checkable facts agree with the dataset (67%).
- **New places** `PL1112` to `PL1137` (26; OpenStreetMap Nominatim): the Nineteenth Army command posts of Anlage 4 from Dettenbach to Pfunds and the corps and division command posts of 1 and 9 April.
- **New claims** `C2248` to `C2274` (27; 2 at 0.3 and the rest at 0.5; each note carries old value none, new value and reason): the Nineteenth Army command post at Dettenbach on 1 April and its eleven moves to Jungingen, Mühlingen, Ravensburg, Schlier, Kempten, Burgberg, Lermoos, Ehrwald, Karrösten, Pians and Pfunds; the LXIV Corps at Ödsbach (1 April) and Enzklösterle (9 April) and near Landeck on 5 May (0.3, sketch); the XVIII SS Corps at St. Trudpert (1 April) and Schonach (21 April); the LXXX Corps at Neckarrems (9 April) and Münsingen (22 April, 0.3, sketch); the Twenty-fourth Army at Engen (1 April) and Eglofs (26 April); the 716th Division at Waldrennach, the 559th at Mundelsheim, the 198th at Kleinbottwar and the 47th at Monrepos; the 16th Volksgrenadier Division assembled south of Gültlingen (18 April); the 189th Division attack from Laichingen (22 April).
  - Parent blank on all 27 (inferred value: old none, new blank, reason: no command relation from a single manuscript).
- **No existing row changed.** No relation, no conflict group (next `CR1067`, `G034`).
- **Coordinate exceptions:** two speed exceptions added for `C1217` (a Lexikon table row with a representative point at Donaueschingen on 26 April) against the new command posts `C2254` (Kempten) and `C2255` (Burgberg); old value none, new value an explained exception, reason: the table row is not a headquarters position.
- **Disagreements recorded, no value changed:** Brandenberger taking command on 1 April (arrived 28 March) in B-745 against 26 March in `C1190`; the LXXX Corps under the Nineteenth Army from 9 April in B-745 against 8 April in `C2123`; the XVIII SS Corps staff dissolved at 2200 on 26 April in B-745 against 27 April in `C1273`; the Twenty-fourth Army shipping its troops across Lake Constance in the night to 26 April in B-745 against 21 April in `C1219`.
- **Semantic scenario 51.**

### B-151 (Manteuffel, Fifth Panzer Army, September-16 December 1944, English translation from the Internet Archive)

- **New reference** `R780` (B-151; archive_document, family `fms_german_manuscript`). Reliability: 6 of 8 checkable facts agree with the dataset (75%).
- **New places** `PL1138` to `PL1142` (5; OpenStreetMap Nominatim): Le Hohwald, Manderscheid in the Bernkastel-Wittlich district, Dackscheid, Kyllburg, Gerolstein.
- **New claims** `C2275` to `C2281` (7; 3 at 0.3 and the rest at 0.5; each note carries old value none, new value and reason): Fifth Panzer Army headquarters at Le Hohwald on Manteuffel's arrival (11 September), at Königshoven on the Aachen front (22 October), the operations staff to Manderscheid (23 November) and the forward move to Dackscheid (15 December); the LVIII Panzer Corps at Eisenschmitt (23 November, 0.3, approximate day); the XLVII Panzer Corps staff complete at Kyllburg (8 December, 0.3, arrival); the LXVI Corps headquarters at Gerolstein (4 November, 0.3, corps number printed as 65).
  - Parent blank on all 7 (inferred value: old none, new blank, reason: no command relation from a single manuscript).
- **New place rather than reuse:** `PL1066` (Manderscheid, used by `C2179`) has the coordinates of the Manderscheid near Arzfeld in the western Eifel; B-151 places the army staff near Eisenschmitt and Gerolstein, which fits Manderscheid in the Bernkastel-Wittlich district, so the new row uses the new place `PL1139` and `PL1066` and `C2179` are unchanged (open point).
- **No existing row changed.** No relation, no conflict group (next `CR1067`, `G034`).
- **Disagreements recorded, no value changed:** the army staff leaving the Aachen sector on 23 November in B-151 against 15 November in `C0956` and 30 November in `C2179`.
- **Semantic scenario 52.**
