- **Result for the five groups:** `G021` preferred/alternate (open), `G022` preferred/alternate/alternate (settled), `G023` disputed/disputed (open), `G024` preferred/alternate (settled), `G025` disputed/disputed (open). 24 conflict groups in all.
### Item 2: the 243rd Division (`C0197` against `C0799` and `C0757`)

Open question before: `C0197` (29 June 1944) had the division destroyed at Cherbourg (`destroyed` true, `disestablished` true) while the CMH rows `C0799` (3 July, parts in line) and `C0757` (24 July, battered remnants near Lessay) show it still fighting; no flag had been changed. Sources read again: the Lexikon der Wehrmacht page of the division (`R196`), the CMH (Breakout and Pursuit ch. 4 p. 60, ch. 12 p. 227) and the English Wikipedia article (`R043`).

- **`C0197`: five fields changed, with old and new values in its notes.** `event_type` destruction -> surrender; `destroyed` true -> false; `disestablished` true -> false; `location_represents` main_body -> sector_center, with a new `location_qualifier` (the 922nd Grenadier Regiment and the elements only; old value: empty); `current_activity` "surrendering in the Cherbourg fortress; destroyed" -> "elements of the division surrendering in the Cherbourg fortress". Date (29 June, approximate), place (Cherbourg), confidence (0.3) and references unchanged except that `R196` was added. Reason: the Lexikon page has the division largely smashed by early July with only the 922nd Grenadier Regiment pushed back to Cherbourg, its last remnants fighting north of Coutances in early August and its official dissolution on 12 September 1944; the CMH rows show it in line on 3 and 24 July; Wikipedia ("last elements lost in the fall of Cherbourg") does not date the end of the division as a whole.
- **`C0799`, `C0757`: Review sentence added (no value changed)** saying that the open point is settled by the change to `C0197`.
- **Not done, recommendation:** the Lexikon dissolution of 12 September 1944 (and the commander list to 26 September in Wikipedia, which disagrees) is not recorded as a row; a row would need a new claim id (`C1278`) with an approximate place (the remnants had been pulled back to the Somme) and confidence 0.3 or 0.5. The unit stays `active` and `CR0010` (LXXXIV Corps, assigned, open) is unchanged. Scott decides whether to add the row.
### Item 3: the parent of the 353rd Division (`CR0615`)

The question was recorded as LXXIV Corps (CMH Siegfried Line ch. 4 p. 87) against LXXXIV Corps (Nafziger 944GIAB, 16 September 1944); the data never had an LXXX Corps reading for September (the LXXX Corps is the parent from 15 October, `CR0657`).

- **`CR0615` note extended; no value changed (parent LXXIV Corps, 17 September to 14 October 1944, inferred).** Old value of the open question: annotated only. New: settled for the LXXIV Corps. Reason: the LXXXIV Corps staff was captured on 21 August 1944 and the corps dissolved on 2 November (Lexikon, `R248`); CMH ch. 4 p. 83 names the 347th and 89th Divisions in the LXXIV Corps and the Nafziger file puts the 347th and 353rd under its "LXXXIV", which fits a misprint of LXXIV; CMH ch. 4 p. 70 has the 353rd nominally under the LXXXI Corps before, and p. 87 and p. 92 have it transferred from the LXXXI to the LXXIV Corps. The Lexikon table (LXXXVIII Corps, First Parachute Army, Maastricht, September) is contradicted by the CMH and not followed. No row was added for the Nafziger reading; the start stays at 17 September (inferred; the CMH gives no day).
### Item 4: stray file `WorldWar2/before.txt`

- **Removed** (`before.txt` at the root of the working copy, `WorldWar2/before.txt` in the GitHub tree; 20,106 bytes). It is a saved text output of `who_controls.py` (Fourth Armored Division, July and August 1944) committed by accident with `02e7272` on 26 October 2026. Checked before removal: no script (`scripts/`, the tools of the publication step, the workflow), schema or document reads or names it; the only mention is the older changelog entry that says it was "left alone"; it is not in `.gitignore`, not in the build, and not listed in `docs/review`. Old value: tracked file; new value: removed. Reason: stray output, no function. No copy was in the Drive folder.
### Documents and tests

- `data/coordinate_exceptions.csv`, the reason of the `C0815`/`C0816` conflict: old text "both rows are kept disputed and no winner is chosen without a source"; new text: both rows are kept, `C0815` preferred and `C0816` alternate since 28 October 2026, with the CMH reference. Reason: the status change of `G024` (Item 1).
- `scripts/semantic_tests.py`: scenario 13 added (7 expectations: statuses of `G021` to `G025`, the flags of `C0197`, the parent of the 353rd Division on 20 September 1944); 201 expectations in all.
- `docs/HANDOFF.md`: state, conflict-group counts (preferred 1,242, alternate 19, disputed 16; the earlier figures 1,157, 16 and 21 were out of date), the conflict bullets and the open-item lists updated; `docs/roadmap.md`: open points marked as settled or still open. README counts unchanged (no claim, unit, relation, place or reference count moved; reference ids now run to `R667`).

## german-1945-batch2-apr-may: German 1945 Batch 2, German units, 1 April to 8 May 1945 (28 October 2026)

Branch `german-1945-batch2-apr-may`, cut from `main` (`6d6bf99`, PR #24); compare it with `main` (`https://github.com/scottstarsman/UnitLocationData/compare/main...german-1945-batch2-apr-may`). Scope approved by Scott (28 October 2026: "Go ahead and start the next batch"): the second and last German 1945 batch, the end of the war in Europe.

- **Claims:** 83 added, `C1195` to `C1277`, dated 1 April to 8 May 1945 (April 69, May 14; confidence 0.9 for 2, 0.7 for 13, 0.5 for 33, 0.3 for 35), for 53 units. One existing claim changed (see below).
- **Units:** 6 placeholders became active: Armee-Abteilung von Lüttwitz (`GER-AA-LUETTW`), Eleventh Army (`GER-ARMY-011A`), Twenty-fourth Army (`GER-ARMY-024A`), OKW (`GER-OKW-000H`), XVIII SS Corps (`GER-SSCOR-018C`) and the 6th SS Mountain Division Nord (`GER-SSGEB-006D`). 6 units added: Twelfth Army (`GER-ARMY-012A`), Army Blumentritt (`GER-ARMY-BLUMT`), Panzer Division von Clausewitz (`GER-PZ-CLAUS`), Division Scharnhorst (`GER-INF-SCHRN`), Division Ulrich von Hutten (`GER-INF-HUTTN`) and the 2nd Mountain Division (`GER-GEB-002D`). The unit table has 367 units, 253 active.
- **Places:** 26 new, `PL0639` to `PL0664` (Lippstadt, the Ruhr pocket, Bavaria, northwest Germany and the Odenwald, Bavarian-Bohemian frontier, Harz, Lake Constance and Black Forest representative points; Timeloberg near Wendisch Evern, Würzburg, Karlsbad, Mühlhausen, Blankenburg, Roßlau, Siegen, Bregenz, Winterberg, Tübingen, Oldenburg, Lauenburg, Berlin, Uelzen, Hallenberg, Bad Dürrheim and Limburg), coordinates from OpenStreetMap Nominatim (`R002`); the representative points are marked as such with a precision of 25 or 50 km.
- **References:** 7 new, `R660` to `R666`: Avalon Project instrument of surrender signed at Lüneburg on 4 May 1945 (`R660`) and the Act of Military Surrender signed at Berlin on 8 May 1945 (`R666`), the English Wikipedia article "German surrender at Lüneburg Heath" (`R661`, for the Timeloberg place only), and the Lexikon pages for the 11., 12. and 15. Armee and the 2. Gebirgs-Division (`R662` to `R665`). CMH The Last Offensive ch. 14 to 20, the Lexikon commander lists and the 1945 tables of pages already registered were also used (same family: Tessin).
- **Relations:** 28 new, `CR0816` to `CR0843`; 53 new evidence links (1,945 in all); no new conflict group (next `G028`). Next free ids: `C1278`, `CR0844`, `PL0665`, `R667`.
- **Operations:** none added. The Ruhr rows carry `OP-RUHR-POCKET`, the Harz rows `OP-HARZ-POCKET`, the Nuremberg rows `OP-NUREMBERG`, the rest `OP-CAMP-CENTRAL-EUROPE`; German claims under these operations (whose `nationality_scope` is Allied) validated without an exception row.
- **Coordinate exception:** one row added to `data/coordinate_exceptions.csv` (`C1255` against `C1254`: the OKW signing places Reims and Berlin, 749 km in one day, are not a headquarters move).
- **Semantic test:** scenario 12 (20 expectations; 194 in all).
- **Change to an existing claim (old value, new value, reason):** `C0937` (Army Group B, 17 April 1945): the Location caveat contained the unfilled format placeholder "(%s, 15 April)C0936"; it now reads "(row C0936, 15 April)". No other text, date, place or flag of an existing claim changed.
- **Changes to existing relations (old value, new value, reason).** All 27 are closures of earlier open relations; each carries the old value, new value and reason in its note (the closure sentence starts "Closed on"). 3 at a sourced date (`narrative`, linked to the end row), 6 at the end of resistance in the Ruhr pocket on 18 April (`inferred`: CMH ch. 16 p. 372 gives the day for the pocket, not for the unit), 18 inferred from the next claim.

## german-1945-batch1-jan-mar: German 1945 Batch 1, German units, 1 January to 31 March 1945 (28 October 2026)

Branch `german-1945-batch1-jan-mar`, cut from `main` (`85c55ee`, PR #23); compare it with `main` (`https://github.com/scottstarsman/UnitLocationData/compare/main...german-1945-batch1-jan-mar`). Scope approved by Scott (German 1945 coverage in two batches; this is the first).

- **Claims:** 119 added, `C1076` to `C1194`, dated 1 January to 28 March 1945 (confidence 0.7 for 10, 0.5 for 71, 0.3 for 38), for 76 units. No existing claim changed.
- **Units:** 6 placeholders became active: Army Group Oberrhein (`GER-AG-OBRHG`), XIV SS Corps (`GER-SSCOR-014C`), 2nd, 7th and 8th Fallschirmjäger Divisions and the 416th Infantry Division. No unit added.
- **Places:** 12 new, `PL0627` to `PL0638` (Erstein, Lauterbourg, Hatten, Cernay, Mulhouse, Wesel, Brandscheid, Erkelenz, Elsdorf, Mönchengladbach, Rees, Freiburg im Breisgau), coordinates from OpenStreetMap Nominatim (`R002`).
- **References:** 4 new, `R656` to `R659` (Lexikon 8. Fallschirmjäger-Division, 5. Panzerarmee and 1. Fallschirm-Armee; CMH The Last Offensive ch. 5). The other Last Offensive chapters, Riviera and Ardennes chapters were already registered.
- **Relations:** 59 new, `CR0757` to `CR0815`; 99 new evidence links (1,892 in all); one new conflict group, `G027` (the First Parachute Army, Blumentritt's arrival: 28 March in CMH, preferred; 20 March in the Lexikon, alternate); next `G028`.
- **Operation:** no operation added. `OP-VERITABLE` is still missing; the February Roer and Reichswald rows carry `OP-CAMP-RHINELAND`.
- **Semantic test:** scenario 11 (15 expectations; 174 in all).

The answer grid before and after is compared in `grid_german_1945_batch1.md`.

German 1945 Batch 2 (April and May 1945) is not started.

## huertgen-roer-operations: operation records for the Hürtgen Forest and the Roer plain, and nine claim links (28 October 2026)

Branch `huertgen-roer-operations`, cut from `main` (`c1ebd61`, which contains German Batch 3 as PR #22); compare it with `main`. Scope approved by Scott on 28 October 2026 ("Yes to all"). Three commits.

- **Documentation only (decisions, no data change):** Scott confirmed the open points of German Batch 3: keep `GER-COR-KVOSG` and `GER-COR-FELDT`; keep both Army Group Oberrhein dates (2 and 10 December) and both Army Group H dates (10 and 11 November); Group Hoehne stays read as the LXXXIX Corps; the Utrecht and Tilburg points stay at 0.3; the Netherlands deception "15. Armee" stays mapped to `GER-ARMY-025A`; the VG and INF id naming stays. Recorded in the HANDOFF decision log.
- **Correction of my earlier wording:** the German Batch 3 roadmap said no Hürtgen or Roer operation existed and that those rows had a blank `operation_id`. No Batch 3 row was blank: they carried `OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`, `OP-QUEEN` (the Roer offensive, already in the table with the short name "Roer") or `OP-CAMP-RHINELAND`. The roadmap line is corrected.
- **Operations (2 new, 51 in all):** `OP-HUERTGEN` (Battle of the Hürtgen Forest, battle, parent `OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`, 1944-11-02 to 1944-12-14, `date_basis` derived: H Hour of the 28th Division's Schmidt attack was 0900 on 2 November, `R642` p. 348; on 14 December the 329th Infantry had put the forest behind and emerged on the Roer plain, `R655` p. 592; the Green Book's Part Five is titled The Huertgen Forest, `R510`) and `OP-ROER-PLAIN` (Battle of the Roer Plain, battle, parent `OP-QUEEN`, 1944-11-16 to 1944-12-09, `official_history`: Part Six title and Map VIII "Drive to the Roer, 16 November-9 December 1944", `R438` ch. 22, `R510`). Both are `Allied` scope, `western_europe`. The windows cover only the fighting dated in this follow-up; the wider Hürtgen fighting (September, and the Roer dams attack of 13 to 15 December, ch. 26) is outside them. No separate Roer dams record was made: no Batch 3 claim belongs to it (`C0977`, 16 December, is Wacht am Rhein).
- **Operation relation (1):** `OP-HUERTGEN` concurrent_with `OP-QUEEN`.
- **References (1):** `R655` (The Siegfried Line Campaign, ch. 25); its page 592 sentence and the ch. 22 map caption were checked on the live pages.
- **Claims relinked (9; only `operation_id` and an annotation in `notes` changed; each annotation says old value, new value and reason):** to `OP-HUERTGEN`: `C0960` (`OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`), `C0964` (`OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`), `C0965` (`OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`), `C0966` (`OP-QUEEN`), `C0967` (`OP-QUEEN`); to `OP-ROER-PLAIN`: `C0963`, `C0968`, `C0969`, `C0972` (all `OP-QUEEN`). Reason in each case: the claim is a Huertgen Forest or Roer plain action inside the window of the narrower battle. No date fell outside a window, so no `operation_exceptions.csv` row was added.
- **Left as they were because membership is unclear (listed, not changed):** `C0958` and `C0961` (VII Corps front at Eschweiler and Stolberg, cited from ch. 18, which the Green Book puts in Part Five but which is not forest fighting), `C0959` (XII SS Corps at Geilenkirchen, overlapping Operation Clipper), `C0962` (47th Volksgrenadier at Eschweiler), `C0971` (3rd Fallschirmjäger at Düren, 26 November, forest edge and plain), `C0955`, `C0956`, `C0957`, `C0970` and `C1045` (before 16 November or month-level; `OP-CAMP-RHINELAND` or `OP-SIEGFRIED-LINE`), `C0977` (`OP-WACHT-AM-RHEIN`).
- **Check:** `validate` 6 warnings (the same as before), 159 semantic expectations, `build --check` and `build_references --check` clean, review sample regenerated, the two audits exit 0; the answer grid is unchanged (9,660 keys, 0 changed, 0 new).

## handoff-branching-rule: HANDOFF status rows after the merge of PR #20 (27 October 2026)

Second commit of `handoff-branching-rule` (the branch is still unmerged, so it carries both related documentation commits). **Documentation only; no data, build, schema or script changed.** `docs/HANDOFF.md`: the claims row no longer says the data sit on the German Batch 2 branches (now `main` `ba961f8` after PR #20); the status rows for German Batch 2 and its follow-up say "merged into `main` with PR #20" instead of "pushed, not merged"; the decision-log line on the follow-up's branching says Scott merged both; the German coverage line counts 89 active German units (was 86) and 123 German placeholders (was 130), and names the follow-up. Counts and next ids (954 claims; C0955, CR0674, PL0586, R641, G027) were already current. Left as written because they are historical or older than this merge: the "State of branches at handoff" list, the Batch 4 and Batch 5 open-item texts and the housekeeping list in section 7. Approved by Scott on 27 October 2026 ("Go ahead").

## handoff-branching-rule: branching rule in HANDOFF section 3 (27 October 2026)

Branch `handoff-branching-rule`, cut from `main` (`ba961f8`, PR #20). **Documentation only; no data, build, schema or script changed.** `docs/HANDOFF.md` section 3 (rules 1, 2, 3 and 5), the task-start line in section 8 (startup checklist step 6) and a decision-log entry: related batches go on one branch with each logical item in its own commit (Scott merges it once; no second branch stacked on an unmerged one for related work), unrelated tasks branch from current `main`, and Scott deletes branches himself. All other standing rules are unchanged. Approved by Scott on 27 October 2026 ("Yes").

## german-batch2-followup-model-lxxx: German Batch 2 follow-up, Model / Army Group B and the LXXX Corps (27 October 2026)

Branch `german-batch2-followup-model-lxxx`, cut from `german-batch2-sep-oct-1944`. **The compare is against `german-batch2-sep-oct-1944`** (`https://github.com/scottstarsman/UnitLocationData/compare/german-batch2-sep-oct-1944...german-batch2-followup-model-lxxx`); compared with `main` it also shows Batch 2. After Batch 2 is merged into `main`, the follow-up can be merged normally (its commits sit on top of the Batch 2 commits). Approved by Scott on 27 October 2026 ("Yes"). Every fact of the research note was read again on the cited page before it was recorded.

- **Claims:** 22 added, `C0933` to `C0954` (confidence 0.7 for 2, 0.5 for 14, 0.3 for 6). Army Group B (`GER-AG-00BG`): `C0933` (17 August 1944, arrival at La Roche-Guyon, 0.7), `C0934` (14 September, Oosterbeek, 0.5), `C0935` (27 October, Fichtenhain, 0.7), `C0936` (15 April 1945, Wuppertal, 0.3), `C0937` (17 April 1945, Haan, disestablished true / destroyed false, 0.3). LXXX Corps (`GER-COR-080C`): `C0938` (5 Sep, 0.5), `C0939` (14 Sep), `C0940` (16 Sep), `C0941` (18 Sep, `G026` preferred), `C0942` (13 Oct, `G026` alternate, 0.3), `C0943` (26 Nov), `C0944` (16 Dec), `C0946` (2 Mar 1945, 0.3), `C0947` (22 Mar), `C0948` (2 Apr), `C0949` (19 Apr, 0.3). Seventh Army `C0945` (2 March 1945, Army Group G, 0.5). Divisions: `C0950` Panzer Lehr and `C0951` 5th Fallschirmjäger (16 Sep 1944), `C0952` 353rd (26 November, 0.3), `C0953` 212th Volksgrenadier and `C0954` 276th Volksgrenadier (both 16 December, 0.5; the 212th is under the LXXX Corps from the NAF listing of 26 November, `CR0672`).
- **Units activated:** `GER-COR-080C`, `GER-VG-212D`, `GER-VG-276D` (placeholder to active via `promote_placeholders.py`).
- **Places:** 7 new (`PL0579` Fichtenhain, `PL0580` Wuppertal, `PL0581` Haan, `PL0582` Meuse front Sedan-Montmedy midpoint, `PL0583` Bitburg-Trier midpoint, `PL0584` Ludwigshafen-Speyer midpoint, `PL0585` Heilbronn); coordinates from OpenStreetMap Nominatim (`R002`); midpoints say so in `Location approximate` and carry a precision of 10 or 25 km.
- **References:** 12 new (`R629` to `R640`: EHRI, HistoryNet, battle-of-arnhem.com, Konfliktlandschaften, the two Nafziger files, English and German Wikipedia on Model, the English Wikipedia LXXX Corps page, CMH Ardennes ch. 2 and 9, Siegfried Line ch. 3). battle-of-arnhem.com and Konfliktlandschaften failed live (500, 404); the Internet Archive copies were read and the reference metadata says so. The Nafziger metadata records that the files cite T-410 and the OKW war diary (the research note said T-312).
- **Relations:** 8 new (`CR0666` to `CR0673`): LXXX Corps under the First Army from 5 September 1944 (closed 17 September, inferred), under the Seventh Army from 18 September (closed 20 March 1945, narrative), under the First Army from 21 March 1945 (closed 18 April, inferred), under the Nineteenth Army from 19 April 1945; Seventh Army under Army Group G from 2 March 1945; 5th Fallschirmjäger, 212th and 276th Volksgrenadier Divisions under the LXXX Corps. No overlap rows were needed.
- **Changes to existing records (old value, new value, reason):**
  - `CR0004` (Army Group B under OB West): `valid_to` open, basis `open_at_last_claim` -> `1945-04-17`, basis `narrative`. Reason: the dissolution on 17 April 1945 (CMH Last Offensive p. 369, Lexikon, EHRI, HistoryNet, Konfliktlandschaften, Wikipedia); `C0937` is linked as `asserts_end`.
  - `CR0005` (Seventh Army under Army Group B): `valid_to` open, basis `open_at_last_claim` -> `1945-03-01`, basis `narrative`. Reason: CMH Last Offensive p. 198 (the Seventh Army moves to Army Group G, 2 March 1945); `C0945` is linked as `asserts_end`.
  - `CR0630` (5th Fallschirmjäger Division under the First Parachute Army): `valid_to` open -> `1944-09-15`, `valid_to_earliest` empty -> `1944-09-04`, basis `open_at_last_claim` -> `inferred_from_next_claim`. Reason: `C0951` (16 September) lists the division in the LXXX Corps; no source dates the change, so the day before is used (annotated in the relation note).
  - `C0716` (Army Group B command, 17 August): notes extended; `reference_ids` `R107;R587;R001` -> `R107;R587;R001;R185;R629` (Lexikon and EHRI); `source` extended; confidence unchanged (0.3), group `G018` unchanged.
  - `C0715` (CMH "commander-designate", `G018`), `C0822` (OB West): notes extended (the 14 September clarification, a pointer); no value changed.
  - `C0823` (OB West, Rundstedt 4/5 September): notes extended with the end of Model's OB West stint (CMH Breakout ch. 32 p. 700; English Wikipedia "eighteen days"; German Wikipedia 3 September kept as a disagreement); `reference_ids` `R411;R430;R001` -> `R411;R430;R001;R410;R635;R636`; `source_locator` + `R410: p. 700`; date, place and confidence unchanged.
  - `CR0579`, `CR0615`, `CR0634`, `CR0657`: notes extended only (the 353rd and Panzer Lehr under the LXXX Corps against NAF of 16 September, which lists the 353rd under the LXXXIV Corps and the division staff only); no date, parent or flag changed.
- **Clarification of an earlier finding:** no claim, here or in Batch 2, says Model's Army Group B command ended on 14 September; the data never had that date (the roadmap and HANDOFF already say so). Notes on `C0715`, `C0716` and `C0822` now say it in the rows.
- **Rejected or conflicting, not written:** the Lexikon and English Wikipedia LXXX division lists of 31 August and 31 December (not written as rows), the Wikipedia and Lexikon place "Metz" for the corps, the ww2byday places, the Lexikon end-of-August Panzer Lehr under the LXXX Corps, the 36th Infantry Division (not a unit in the dataset), the German Wikipedia's 3 September end of the OB West stint (kept as a note on `C0823`), and the Lexikon "Oberrhein, vernichtet" end of the LXXX Corps (the last row is 19 April 1945 under the Nineteenth Army).
- **Conflict group:** `G026` (LXXX Corps, `C0941` preferred 18 September, `C0942` alternate October; the groups differ in date and place). `G018` unchanged.
- **Semantic tests:** scenario 9 (`scripts/semantic_tests.py`), 26 expectations; 134 expectations hold in total.
- **Grid:** 8,100 answers before, 8,220 after (120 new keys for the three new units); 1,676 changed, explained in `grid_german_batch2_followup.md`: 1,410 certainty only (Seventh Army to Army Group B and Army Group B to OB West now "certain" between dated listings), 58 the same plus the direct line, 28 Seventh Army to Army Group G on 5 March 1945, 168 LXXX Corps chains (Panzer Lehr, 5th Fallschirmjäger, 353rd, 48th), 12 text only; no "certain" became "possible", no non-German unit changed.
- **Validator changes:** 6 known warnings (2 new, expected: `C0945` and `C0947` assert the end of a relation on the day after it). `C0939` was given a precision of 10 km instead of 5 km (the validator warns about a corps sector recorded as an exact front-line point); this is a value of a new row, changed before publication.
- **Checks:** `validate.py` (OK: 196 claim files, 954 claims, 359 units, 23 conflict groups), `semantic_tests.py` (134 expectations hold), `build.py --check`, `build_references.py --check`, `review_sample.py --check`, `check_coordinates.py` (3 older warnings; 7 explained exceptions), `audit_command_location.py` and `audit_temporal_relationships.py` (41 `parent_change`, one new: `GER-FJ-005D`), `check_claim_ids.py --base german-batch2-sep-oct-1944` and `--base main` (no id reused or dropped; next free `C0955`), every quotation of the 22 claims re-searched on the cited pages (90 of 90 found; `spot_check_german_batch2_followup.md`).

## german-batch2-sep-oct-1944: German Batch 2, 1 September to 31 October 1944 (27 October 2026)

German units at division level and above for the Seine retreat, the Siegfried Line, Aachen, Lorraine, the Netherlands and the Vosges. Branch `german-batch2-sep-oct-1944`, branched from `main` (`5da3a3c`, which holds Batch 5, German Batch 1 and German Batch 1b, merged by PR #18 and PR #19 on 27 October 2026); compare it with `main`. Approved by Scott on 27 October 2026 ("Sounds good. Let's to batch 2").

- **Claims:** 111 added, `C0822` to `C0932` (confidence 0.3 for 54, 0.5 for 49, 0.7 for 8; none higher), for 61 units; 33 placeholders became active (Army Group G, First Parachute Army, First, Fifteenth and Nineteenth Armies, LXIV, LXVI, LXXIV, LXXXII, LXXXVIII, XIII SS and XII SS Corps, IV Luftwaffe Field Corps, 3rd and 15th Panzer Grenadier, 11th Panzer, 12th, 246th, 553rd and 559th divisions, and others; the full list is in `docs/roadmap.md`). Rows: command level (OB West, Army Group B, Army Group G, armies), corps, CMH day-level division rows, Schramm rows and Lexikon month-level rows (15th of the month, confidence 0.3, `event_type` other).
- **Places:** 37 new (`PL0541` to `PL0578`), none without a source for its coordinate (Wikipedia coordinates `R001`, OpenStreetMap Nominatim `R002`; regions are stood for by one town with a precision of 25 or 50 km and say so in `Location approximate`).
- **References:** 24 new (`R605` to `R628`). The Lexikon rows cite the content page (`...-R.htm`); the spot check found two rows that cited the frameset page (`...48ID.htm`, `...64ID.htm`), which does not hold the table, and all 16 such references were replaced for the new rows only (older rows keep their references).
- **Relations:** 72 new (`CR0594` to `CR0665`). 24 earlier relations were closed with `valid_to_basis inferred_from_next_claim`, `valid_to` the day before the first row that lists a different higher unit, `valid_to_earliest` the last listing, and an annotated note (old value open, new value the date, the reason): `CR0007`, `CR0008`, `CR0015`, `CR0035`, `CR0038`, `CR0048`, `CR0050`, `CR0560`, `CR0561`, `CR0563`, `CR0564`, `CR0568` to `CR0574`, `CR0576`, `CR0579`, `CR0581`, `CR0582`, `CR0585`, `CR0593`. Of these, `CR0038` (21st Panzer Division under the I SS Panzer Corps, open since Batch 1b) is closed on 20 September because `C0878` (21 September, CMH ch. 5 p. 233) shows the division in the XLVII Panzer Corps; `CR0561` (Seventh Army under the Fifth Panzer Army from 20 August, no end) is closed on 8 September because `C0839` lists the Seventh Army under Army Group B; without that closure the units under the Seventh Army chained through the Fifth Panzer Army to Army Group G (found in the first grid run and fixed).
- **New relation without a corps or army row of its own:** the First Parachute Army to Army Group B (assigned from 4 September, narrative; Lexikon: first combat action "im Verband der Heeresgruppe B"; CMH ch. 8 p. 193), linked to `C0826` to `C0828`; the chains of its units now reach OB West.
- **Operations:** `OP-METZ` added (battle, parent `OP-LORRAINE`, 27 September to 13 December 1944, Wikipedia infobox, `R612`); no Batch 2 claim carries it.
- **Semantic tests:** scenario 8 (`scripts/semantic_tests.py`), 15 expectations: the 21st Panzer Division's parent change on 20/21 September, the 9th and 10th SS under the II SS Panzer Corps on 17 September, the 3rd Panzer Grenadier, 59th, 559th, 116th and 246th Divisions.
- **Grid:** 6,820 answers before, 8,100 after (1,280 new keys); 1,702 changed, explained in `grid_german_batch2.md` (77 text-only, 1,625 with a changed parent, chain level or certain/possible; no answer of a non-German unit changed).
- **Stray file removed:** running `wc_grid.py --help` in the repository root wrote a file named `--help` into the commit "semantic_tests scenario 8"; a later commit removes it (the older `before.txt` from commit `02e7272` is left alone).
- **Tooling notes:** a relation linked to a claim of another unit makes the claim list its own unit as parent (validator error "the unit is listed as its own parent"): use a claim of the subject with role `asserts_start` and `effective_from`; an overlong `current_activity` (over 120 characters) stops the batch scripts; `precision_km` takes only 1, 3, 5, 10, 25 and 50.
- **Checks:** `validate.py` (OK: 193 claim files, 932 claims, 359 units, 22 conflict groups; 4 known warnings), `semantic_tests.py` (108 expectations hold), `build.py --check`, `build_references.py --check`, `review_sample.py --check`, `check_coordinates.py` (3 older warnings), the two audits, `check_claim_ids.py --base main` (no id reused or dropped).

## german-batch1b-jul-1944: further Caen-front divisions, month-level rows and corps lists (27 October 2026)

Third commit of German Batch 1b. **16 claims added (`C0806` to `C0821`); 2 new conflict groups (`G024`: 716th Division; `G025`: 711th Division); `CR0591`/`CR0592` created by the 272nd, 346th and 89th Division rows (see below); 2 relation notes and end dates adjusted.** Old value for every added row: no row. Correction to the second entry of this file: it names `CR0588` to `CR0593`; only `CR0588`, `CR0589` and `CR0590` were created in that commit, and its two new conflict groups are `G022` and `G023` (`G021` is the first commit's).

- **LXXXVI Corps (`C0806`, `C0807`, `C0819`).** 9 July: most of Caen lost (Lexikon), 18 July: Goodwood hits the corps front east of Caen (Lexikon, with the Wikipedia dates 18-20 July), and a month-level row of the corps list in the July 1944 order of battle (Nafziger; 346th, 21st Panzer, 16th Luftwaffe Field Division in `composing_units`; the 711th Division part is in group `G025` and is left out).
- **I SS Panzer Corps and II SS Panzer Corps (`C0820`, `C0821`).** Month-level rows of the Nafziger July 1944 list, 15 July as an approximate date, confidence 0.3, no parent. The row of the II SS Panzer Corps lists the 9th SS, 10th SS and 277th Divisions in `composing_units`; the open decision on `CR0051` and `CR0052` (parent of the 9th and 10th SS Panzer Divisions) is not touched.
- **272nd and 271st Infantry Divisions (`C0808` to `C0811`).** 272nd: enters the line south of Caen on 11 July and has relieved the 1st SS Panzer Division by 16 July (Lexikon; the Nafziger footnote supports the relief). 271st: vanguard at Bernay 12 July, and from 17 July relieving the 10th SS Panzer Division southwest of Caen (Lexikon; Nafziger footnote). The Lexikon page's "130 km ost-südöstlich Lisieux" for Bernay is wrong (about 35 km) and is not used. The 271st rows have no parent (open decision on the II SS Panzer Corps).
- **Month-level rows, 15 July (approximate date, 0.3).** 346th Division in the LXXXVI Corps (`C0812`), 89th Division in the LXXXI Corps at Le Havre (`C0813`), 116th Panzer Division at the disposal of the Fifteenth Army, place "Normandie" (`C0814`, region only; the move into the front is not dated in the sources read).
- **Conflict group `G024` (716th Division).** `C0815` (text of the Lexikon page: coastal sector in southern France by 10 July, headquarters Thuir) against `C0816` (table of the same page: July "Normandie", Seventh Army at disposal). Both disputed; no winner chosen. `G025` (711th Division, July 1944): `C0817` (Lexikon table: LXXXI Corps, Channel coast north of Lisieux) against `C0818` (Nafziger: part of the division in the LXXXVI Corps). Both disputed, no winner chosen.
- **Relations.** `CR0591` 272nd Division under the I SS Panzer Corps from 11 July (claim date; open at the last listing); `CR0592` 346th Division under the LXXXVI Corps from 15 July (approximate date, inferred start); `CR0593` 89th Division under the LXXXI Corps from 15 July (approximate date, inferred start). The 346th Division's older relation to the Fifteenth Army (`CR0011`) is not closed; the two are different echelons and the question is open. `CR0043` (Panzer Lehr under the I SS Panzer Corps) `valid_to_earliest` moved from 27 June to 9 July and `CR0054` (12th SS under the I SS Panzer Corps) from 27 June to 18 July, because new rows list the relation on those days (a `Review (German Batch 1b)` sentence is in each note).
- **No July source read for the 85th Division.** The Lexikon table has May (Fifteenth Army, Channel coast) and then August; the division stays an open point.
- **Semantic tests.** Scenario 6: the expectations for Panzer Lehr on 9 July (now certain, `C0788` lists the relation) and for the 352nd Division on 20 July (II Parachute Corps certain, LXXXIV Corps absent) are replaced, and a 10 July Panzer Lehr expectation is added. New scenario 7 (12 expectations) covers the changed and new relations of this batch.

Second commit of German Batch 1b. **34 claims added (`C0772` to `C0805`), 3 operations (`OP-CHARNWOOD`, `OP-JUPITER`, `OP-GOODWOOD`), 6 command relations (`CR0588` to `CR0593`), 3 new conflict groups (`G022`, `G023`; `G021` from the first commit); earlier relations `CR0012`, `CR0046`, `CR0581`, `CR0584` changed (listed below).** Old value for every added row: no row.

- **Caen front, Lexikon der Wehrmacht, Nafziger, CMH and Schramm (`C0772` to `C0792`).** 1st SS Panzer Division 6, 11, 15 and 18 July (`C0772` to `C0775`: Bretteville-sur-Laize, the Maltot-Caen sector, the Saint-André-sur-Orne to Frénouville line, the Goodwood counterattack); 12th SS Panzer Division 8, 9, 11, 16, 17 and 18 July (`C0776` to `C0781`) and the month-level conflict group `G023` (`C0782` Nafziger: I SS Panzer Corps reserve; `C0783` Lexikon: LXXXVI Corps; both disputed, 0.3); 9th SS 8 and 11 July (`C0784`, `C0785`); 10th SS 17 July (`C0786`, 0.7: Lexikon, Nafziger and Schramm); 21st Panzer 8 July (`C0787`); Panzer Lehr 9 and 10 July (`C0788`, `C0789`) and the conflict group `G022` (`C0790` Lexikon table, June; `C0791` Feldgrau, July; `C0792` Lexikon dated list of 15 July; all disputed, no winner chosen).
- **Cotentin and Vire (`C0793` to `C0805`).** 2nd SS Panzer Division 3, 6, 7 and 9 July (`C0793` to `C0796`), 17th SS Panzer Grenadier Division 4 July (`C0797`), 91st Luftlande and 243rd Divisions 3 July (`C0798`, `C0799`), 353rd 3 July (`C0800`), 3rd Fallschirmjäger 11 and 18 July (`C0801`, `C0804`), 352nd 11 and 18 July (`C0802`, `C0803`), LXXXIV Corps 14 July (`C0805`, three families).
- **Relations added.** `CR0588` 1st SS Panzer Division under the I SS Panzer Corps from 6 July (claim date), ended 5 August (inferred; `CR0570` XLVII Panzer Corps from 6 August); `CR0589` 2nd SS Panzer Division in the Seventh Army reserve 3 to 5 July (`reserve`, `end_unknown true`); `CR0590` 353rd Division under the LXXXIV Corps from 3 July, ended 23 July (inferred; the 24 July row lists the corps reserve `CR0583`).
- **Earlier relations changed (every answer of `who_controls` that moves is in the grid comparison).** `CR0581` (17th SS under the LXXXIV Corps): start moved from 24 July to 4 July (Breakout and Pursuit ch. 5 p. 81; the link of `C0760` becomes `observed_during`). `CR0046` (17th SS under the II Parachute Corps): end moved from 23 July to 3 July. `CR0584` (352nd under the II Parachute Corps): start moved from 24 July to 11 July (ch. 8 p. 148; the link of `C0763` becomes `observed_during`). `CR0012` (352nd under the LXXXIV Corps): end moved from 23 July to 10 July. The `Review (German Batch 1b)` sentence of each relation note says so.
- **Open point, no flag changed.** `C0799` (243rd Division, 3 July) carries the note on the open point `C0197` against `C0757`: parts of the division in line on 3 July (CMH) against the Lexikon, which has the division largely destroyed by early July. Neither `C0197` nor `C0757` was changed.
- **References.** `R599` and `R601` (Lexikon 272nd and 271st Infantry Division pages) completed; the new CMH chapter pages are existing references.

## german-batch1b-jul-1944: command level and corps on 1 July (27 October 2026)

First commit of German Batch 1b (branch `german-batch1b-jul-1944`, from `german-batch1-jul-aug-1944`). **7 claims added (`C0765` to `C0771`), 2 command relations (`CR0586`, `CR0587`), 1 placeholder activated (`GER-COR-086C`), 12 places (`PL0525` to `PL0536`; two more follow), 6 references (`R593` to `R598`); `C0714` joins the new conflict group `G021`; `CR0039` and `CR0587` ended with inferred dates.** Old value for every added row: no row.

- **Command level (`C0765` to `C0767`).** `C0765` OB West, 3 July, Kluge takes over the command at 2000 hours (Schramm, OKW war diary, p. 324, confidence 0.3). `C0766` Panzer Group West, Schramm row of the existing conflict group `G019` (Eberbach for Geyr, listed with the 2 July replacement of Rundstedt; alternate, 0.3). `C0767` Army Group B, 19 July, Schramm chronology (p. 458): Kluge leads the army group "ab 19. 7."; alternate in the new group `G021`, whose preferred row stays `C0714` (17 July, CMH and Wikipedia).
- **Corps on 1 July (`C0768` to `C0771`).** Breakout and Pursuit ch. 2 p. 30 gives the order of the corps from east to west: LXXXVI, I SS Panzer, II SS Panzer, XLVII Panzer, II Parachute, LXXXIV. Four rows: LXXXVI Corps (0.7, with the Lexikon der Wehrmacht page), I SS, II SS and XLVII Panzer Corps (0.5). The text also says that on 28 June Panzer Group West took control of the four corps on the right: new relations `CR0586` (LXXXVI) and `CR0587` (XLVII) under Panzer Group West, `unspecified_subordination`, from 28 June (narrative).
- **Earlier values changed.** `C0714`: `conflict_group_id` blank to `G021` (see above; a Review note). `CR0039` (XLVII Panzer Corps, parent unknown): `valid_to` 1944-08-06 to 1944-06-27 (basis `inferred` with an `asserts_end` link from `C0771`), because ch. 2 p. 30 names the parent from 28 June; the earlier close on 6 August is replaced. `CR0587` is closed on 1944-08-06 (inferred from next claim) because `CR0566` has the corps under the Seventh Army from 7 August.
- **Places.** `PL0525` to `PL0538`: Bretteville-sur-Laize, Maltot, Évrecy, Potigny, Cagny, Montgardon, Bernay, the Maltot-Caen front sector, the Saint-André-sur-Orne to Frénouville line, the Lessay-Périers line, the Caumont-Rauray sector, Normandy front (area named only as Normandie, precision 50 km, a representative point only), Condé-sur-Noireau, Le Havre, Vimont. Midpoints and representative points are described in `coord_source_note`.
- **References.** `R593` Schramm, KTB OKW IV/1 (family `okw_kriegstagebuch_schramm`); `R594` Nafziger 944BGAA; `R595` Feldgrau.com Panzer Lehr (both in the family `lexikon_wehrmacht_de`: Tessin lineage, not independent of the Lexikon); `R596` to `R598` Wikipedia Operation Charnwood, Jupiter and Goodwood.

## german-batch1-jul-aug-1944: spot-check corrections and status documents (27 October 2026)

Fifth and last commit of German Batch 1. **Two claim notes corrected after the spot check (no value of any column except `notes` changed); `README.md`, `docs/roadmap.md` and `docs/HANDOFF.md` updated.**

- **`C0724` (Seventh Army, 7 August), note.** Old text: "the army headquarters had left Le Mans; ch. 22 p. 435". New text: "administrative personnel had been evacuated from Le Mans, long the Seventh Army headquarters, after the loss of Laval; ch. 22 p. 435". Reason: the page says administrative personnel were evacuated, not that the headquarters had left.
- **`C0729` (LXXXI Corps, 12 August), note.** Old text: "the LXXXI Corps, out of contact with most of its divisions, was placed under control of the Fifth Panzer Army". New text: "the LXXXI Corps, which had only radio contact with its remaining units, was placed under control of the Fifth Panzer Army". Reason: ch. 25 p. 502-503 says the headquarters was severed from its divisions in the Argentan sector and had only radio contact with the remaining units.
- **Status documents.** `README.md`: 764 claims, 156 of 359 units active. `docs/roadmap.md`: status line, batch-table row German 1 and a German Batch 1 section (done, not covered, six open points for Scott). `docs/HANDOFF.md`: current-state table (claims 764: US 573, Germany 127, UK 60, Canada 4; July 74, August 176; units 359, 156 active, 203 placeholders; relations 584; places 518; references 586; operations 45; conflict groups 17; next free ids `C0765`, `CR0586`, `PL0525`, `R593`, `G021`), the German coverage line, the baselines (validator 156 claim files, 764 claims, 359 units, 17 groups; 79 semantic expectations; grid 6,700 answers) and a decision-log entry for the batch. Open points for Scott (also in the roadmap): the dates in `G018` to `G020`, the Hausser successor, the 243rd Division flag, the two Panzer Group West records, and the 12th SS parent before 19 August.

## german-batch1-jul-aug-1944: semantic tests for the German parent changes (27 October 2026)

Fourth commit of German Batch 1. **`scripts/semantic_tests.py` gets a sixth scenario (`scenario_6_german_parent_changes_1944`, 11 new expectations; 68 become 79).** It records what `who_controls` answers today for the undated German parent changes of July and August 1944: Panzer Lehr (I SS Panzer Corps, then the Seventh Army, then the LXXXIV Corps), the XLVII Panzer Corps (Seventh Army, Panzer Group Eberbach, Seventh Army again), the 12th SS Panzer Division (I SS Panzer Corps to the LXXXIV Corps) and the 352nd Division (LXXXIV Corps to the II Parachute Corps). Days between two listings are `possible`, never `certain`. The 68 older expectations hold unchanged. No data changed.

## german-batch1-jul-aug-1944: German order of battle on 24 July and the 275th at Barenton (27 October 2026)

Third commit of German Batch 1. **8 claims added (`C0757` to `C0764`), 6 command relations (`CR0580` to `CR0585`), 1 place (`PL0524`, Périers), 2 placeholders activated (`GER-FJ-005D`, `GER-INF-275D`); 3 earlier relations closed with inferred ends.** Old value for every added row: no row. Source: Breakout and Pursuit ch. 12 p. 227 (the German order of battle on the morning of 24 July, the day before Cobra), ch. 24 p. 459 and ch. 25 p. 482 (R592 for ch. 12; the other chapters already cited).

- **24 July (`C0757` to `C0763`, all 0.5):** the 243rd Division and the 91st Luftlande Division near Lessay (the 91st controlling remnants of the 77th and the 265th Kampfgruppe), the 2nd SS Panzer Division with the 6th Parachute Regiment and the 17th SS Panzer Grenadier Division in the Périers area, the 5th Parachute Division east of them with one regiment, the 353rd Division as LXXXIV Corps reserve south of Périers, and the 352nd Division under the II Parachute Corps on the west bank of the Vire. Points are the sector places named in the source, not command posts; each row says so.
- **Relations.** The LXXXIV Corps controlled the 2nd SS Panzer Division, the 17th SS Panzer Grenadier Division and the 5th Parachute Division (`unspecified_subordination`; the source says controlled) and held the 353rd as `reserve` (`CR0583`, ended 18 August by the II Parachute Corps relation `CR0573`, `end_unknown true`). `CR0012` (352nd Division under the LXXXIV Corps since 6 June) and `CR0046` (17th SS Panzer Grenadier Division under the II Parachute Corps since 10 June) are closed on 23 July with inferred ends and `change_undated true`: the Green Book shows the 352nd under the II Parachute Corps (ch. 12 p. 227, ch. 17 p. 326) and the 17th SS under the LXXXIV Corps, but no page dates either change. The 2nd SS relation to the LXXXIV Corps (`CR0580`) ends on 6 August, the day before the 7 August relation to the XLVII Panzer Corps (`CR0569`).
- **275th Division (`C0764`, 6 August, Barenton):** the Green Book calls its defenses at Barenton weak on the evening of 6 August; parent not verified (`unknown` relation `CR0585`).
- **Open point.** The 243rd Division row (`C0757`) shows remnants near Lessay on 24 July although Wikipedia lists the division as destroyed in June 1944 (claim `C0197`, `destroyed true`, `disestablished true`). Both rows are kept and no flag was changed. Scott decides whether `C0197` should lose its destroyed flag or carry a conflict group.

## german-batch1-jul-aug-1944: corps, panzer and infantry divisions, July and August 1944 (27 October 2026)

Second commit of German Batch 1. **30 claims added (`C0727` to `C0756`), 18 command relations (`CR0562` to `CR0579`), 13 places (`PL0511` to `PL0523`), 2 references (`R591` Breakout and Pursuit ch. 7, `R592` ch. 12), 11 placeholders activated (`GER-COR-081C`, `GER-PZ-058C`, `GER-PZ-009D`, `GER-PZ-116D`, `GER-SSPZ-001D`, `GER-SSPZ-002D`, `GER-INF-084D`, `GER-INF-277D`, `GER-INF-353D`, `GER-INF-363D`, `GER-INF-708D`); no new unit records. `build/` and the review sample regenerated.** Old value for every added row: no row. Source: the CMH Green Book Breakout and Pursuit (HyperWar edition, one source family) with the page of the printed book; the page numbers follow the printed numbers at the end of each page, not the HyperWar anchors, some of which are one page off.

- **July (4 claims):** Panzer Lehr's counterattack of 11 July (`C0753`, le Dezert, 0.7, ch. 7 p. 135-137) and its front on 25 July under the Cobra bombardment (`C0754`, point Hebecrevon, 0.5, ch. 12 p. 227-240); the II Parachute Corps feint from Saint-Lo on 11 July (`C0755`, 0.5, ch. 7 p. 137; the Green Book gives the plan and the jump-off time, not the result); the LXXXIV Corps command change on 28 July (`C0756`, Elfeldt for Choltitz, Coutances area, 0.5, ch. 17 p. 328).
- **Mortain attack, 6 to 12 August (13 claims):** 1st SS Panzer Division at Tinchebray on 6 August and at Juvigny on 7 August; 2nd SS Panzer Division capturing Mortain; 2nd Panzer Division halted at le Mesnil-Adelee; the 116th Panzer Division command change (Schwerin relieved at 1600, Col. Walter Reinhard, `C0735`); the XLVII Panzer Corps attack (`C0736`) and arrival at Argentan on 12 August (`C0738`); LVIII Panzer Corps taking over Mortain (`C0739`); LXXXI Corps at Alencon on 6, 11 and 12 August (`C0727` to `C0729`; the last one is a 0.3 row using the 11 August place) with the 9th Panzer Division at Fresnay-sur-Sarthe and the 708th Division at le Mans on 10 August (`C0730`, `C0731`).
- **Falaise pocket, 17 to 20 August (13 claims):** the 116th Panzer Division at le Bourg-Saint-Leonard on 17 August and at Argentan on 20 August; the breakout of the II Parachute Corps (`C0746`), the 3rd Parachute Division (`C0747`, Schimpf wounded, Meindl in command), the 353rd Division (`C0745`), the XLVII Panzer Corps (`C0741`), the 2nd Panzer Division at Saint-Lambert (`C0742`), the 1st SS Panzer Division at Chambois (`C0744`), the 12th SS Panzer Division infantry group (`C0749`), the 277th Division (`C0750`), the 84th and 363rd Divisions in the Bois de Feuillet (`C0751`, `C0752`), and Elfeldt's surrender (`C0748`).
- **Relations.** All new subordinations in the Green Book are worded 'under', 'placed under control of' or 'committed', so they are `unspecified_subordination`; the LXXIV Corps divisions are `assigned` (the source calls them its divisions). Dated intervals: XLVII Panzer Corps under the Seventh Army 7 to 11 August (`CR0566`), under Panzer Group Eberbach 12 to 18 August (`CR0567`, inferred from the account of 12 August and the corps list on p. 528) and under the Seventh Army again from 19 August (`CR0572`); Panzer Lehr under the Seventh Army 11 to 23 July (`CR0578`) and under the LXXXIV Corps from 24 July (`CR0579`). Three earlier relations were closed with an inferred end (all `change_undated true`): `CR0039` (XLVII Panzer Corps, parent unknown, to 6 August), `CR0043` (Panzer Lehr under I SS Panzer Corps, to 10 July) and `CR0054` (12th SS Panzer Division under I SS Panzer Corps, to 19 August). The LXXXI Corps (6 to 11 August) and the LVIII Panzer Corps (from 12 August) have `unknown` parents (`CR0562`, `CR0571`); the LXXXI Corps is placed under the Fifth Panzer Army from 12 August (`CR0563`).
- **Review notes.** Several corps rows put the point on a town named for the sector, not for a command post; each says so in a `Location approximate` or `Location caveat` sentence. `C0741` and `C0744` rest on 'probably' wording in the Green Book and say so. Elfeldt's rank and the 12th SS commander's rank are given as the Green Book gives them (`Lt. Col. Hubert Meyer` appears as Oberstleutnant in the commander field).

## german-batch1-jul-aug-1944: higher headquarters, 2 July to 20 August 1944 (27 October 2026)

First commit of German Batch 1 (Scott approved German units from 30 June to 31 August 1944 on 27 October 2026). **14 claims added (`C0713` to `C0726`), 3 command relations (`CR0559` to `CR0561`), 1 place (`PL0510`, the Falaise pocket), 1 new unit record (`GER-PZG-EBERB`, Panzer Group Eberbach), 1 placeholder activated (`GER-PZA-005A`, Fifth Panzer Army), 3 conflict groups (`G018` to `G020`), 5 references (`R586` to `R590`: Breakout and Pursuit ch. 2, 8 and 17; Wikipedia, Oberbefehlshaber West and Heinrich Eberbach); `build/` and the review sample regenerated.** Old value for every added row: no row.

- **OB West (`C0713`, 2 July):** Kluge replaces Rundstedt (ch. 2 p. 28, ch. 3 p. 47; Wikipedia list). Place is the headquarters of `C0013` used as a representative point, confidence 0.3 (`nearest_earlier_command_post`, `location_date` 1944-06-06).
- **Army Group B (`C0714`, 17 July; `C0715`/`C0716`, Model, group `G018`):** Rommel's injury and Kluge taking over (ch. 8 p. 169). **`G018`, inferred value:** the Green Book says Model, still the commander-designate at 0600 on 18 August, assumed command 'at midnight after a day of inspection' (ch. 27 p. 532, 535); the row reads that as the end of 18 August (19 August, inferred); Wikipedia says 17 August. Both rows `disputed`.
- **Panzer Group West (`C0717`/`C0718`, Eberbach for Geyr, group `G019`; `C0719`/`C0720`, renamed Fifth Panzer Army, group `G020`):** the Green Book gives 4 July (Geyr relieved two days after Rundstedt) and 5 August; Wikipedia gives 2 July and 1 August. The CMH rows are `preferred`, the Wikipedia rows `alternate`. Place Thury-Harcourt as a representative point (confidence 0.3). The two names stay two unit records (`GER-PZG-WESTA`, `GER-PZA-005A`; unit notes updated).
- **Panzer Group Eberbach (`C0721`, 12 August; new unit `GER-PZG-EBERB`):** provisional headquarters formed for the second Avranches attack, directly under Army Group B (`CR0559`, `unspecified_subordination`: the source says only 'directly under'); first documented date 12 August (ch. 25 p. 483, 502).
- **Fifth Panzer Army (`C0722`, 18 August):** command post near Lisieux, Model's conference (ch. 27 p. 532); `CR0560` to Army Group B from the claim date.
- **Seventh Army (`C0723`, 31 July, Le Mans, Kluge in direct command; `C0724`, 7 August, the Luttich attack; `C0725`/`C0726`, 20 August, Hausser wounded and the army subordinated to the Fifth Panzer Army, `CR0561`).** The successor to Hausser is stated differently (Funck, ch. 27 p. 557; Eberbach, ch. 31 p. 663 and Wikipedia): recorded as an open point in `C0725`, not resolved.

## batch5-apr-may-1945: unit records for X Corps and XIV Corps (27 October 2026)

Ninth commit of Batch 5 (open point 3, approved by Scott on 27 October 2026). **`data/units.csv`: 2 rows added (356 to 358 units; 215 to 217 placeholders); `data/references_meta.csv`: 1 reference added (R585, 579 references); `data/claims/us/US-INF-095D.csv`: the note of C0647 changed. No claim, relation or place was added, removed or re-dated.**

- **`US-COR-010C` (X Corps), new row:** `record_status` placeholder, `theater` pacific, `formed_date` 1942-05-15, `formed_date_source` https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X_Corps_(United_States) (R585). Old value: no row. Reason: the CMH ETO-OB table of the 4th Armored Division lists "X" in the Corps column in the row of 9 Apr 45 and the Combat Chronicle repeats it; the unit record gives that table entry an id. The `formed_date` is the activation date the text of the Wikipedia page gives (15 May 1942, Sherman, Texas); the infobox gives 1 May 1942 as the start of the active span and is not used.
- **`US-COR-014C` (XIV Corps), new row:** `record_status` placeholder, `theater` pacific, `formed_date` blank (no source). Old value: no row. Reason: the CMH ETO-OB table of the 95th Infantry Division lists "XIV" in the Corps column in the row of 4 Apr 45. The Wikipedia page gives 1 October 1933 only as the constitution date of the reserve headquarters and the sentence that dates the start of the wartime history is garbled in the page ("from J 1942"), so no activation date is stated and none is inferred.
- **Finding, reported to Scott:** the sources read (Wikipedia, X Corps and XIV Corps) place both corps only in the Pacific in the Second World War, so the two table entries are not confirmed for Europe and may be misprints. The `theater` of both records is therefore `pacific` (the value the sources give, not an inference about Europe), and the `unit_note` of each says so. No source read says where the headquarters stood in April 1945; nothing is invented. **Open for Scott:** whether to keep them as identity-only records (done), or to remove them once a source identifies the corps the table meant.
- **Relations:** none to X Corps or XIV Corps existed (checked: no `US-COR-010C` or `US-COR-014C` in `data/command_relations.csv` or `data/command_relation_claims.csv`), so no placeholder relation needed fixing; none was added. The table rows (4th Armored Division, 9 to 16 April; 95th Division, 4 to 8 April) remain unrecorded; the 95th Division relations stay as decided (Combat Chronicle: XIX Corps to 8 April, XVI Corps from 9 April).
- **`US-INF-095D` C0647, `notes`:** old clause "XIV Corps has no unit record in this dataset, so no relation to it is recorded, and the table-based relation..."; new clause "no relation to XIV Corps is recorded, and the table-based relation..." plus a `Review` sentence with old and new text and the reason (a unit record now exists; the sources read place the corps only in the Pacific).
- **Derived files:** `build/coverage.csv` (2 rows), `docs/coverage.md` (US corps: 2 placeholders, 17 units, 7 with formed_date; all: 217 placeholders, 358 units, 236 with formed_date), `build/unit_references.csv` (1 row), `data/references.csv` (R585), `build/all_claims.csv` (the C0647 note), `docs/review/README.md` (counts only). `scripts/semantic_tests.py`: no expectation changed.
- **Checked:** `validate.py` (OK, 141 claim files, 712 claims, 358 units, 14 conflict groups; the three known warnings), `build.py --check`, `build_references.py --check`, `review_sample.py --check`.

## batch5-apr-may-1945: relations closed by the CMH table end (27 October 2026)

Seventh commit of Batch 5, found by a check after the three claim commits. **`data/command_relations.csv`: 9 existing open relations closed with the end the CMH ETO-OB table gives; `data/command_relation_claims.csv`: 10 links changed to carry the end; the claim notes that print the relation dates and the build files were regenerated. No claim, place or unit was added or removed.** The relation planner closed an open relation only when a new claim listed a replacement of the same kind; these 9 had no replacement in the dataset (the unit moved to a higher unit that has no record, or the table simply stops listing it), so they would have stayed open past the table end (for example the 100th Division's VI Corps relation after the table's row of 25 April 1945, which lists no corps). Rule applied: the end is the day before the first table row that no longer lists the entry (the table-end rule used for `CR0447`); where a Batch 5 claim of the unit lists the relation on a date up to the end, that claim's link carries `asserts_end` (or `asserts_start_and_end`) with `stated_effective_to` and basis `assignment_table` (the same pattern as `CR0447` in the corps follow-up), `end_unknown` false; where none does, the end is recorded as `inferred_from_next_claim` with `valid_to_earliest` the last claim that listed it. Each relation carries the old, new and reason in its own note. Where a table lists an entry in more than one run (for example the 2nd Division's V Corps), the relation is closed at the last end and the note says the gaps are not modeled, as for the other table relations. The relations changed:
  - `CR0130` 3rd Armored Division to First Army (assigned): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-04-30 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0664; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 30 April 1945 (first row without it 1 May 45); C0664 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-04-30); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0203` 1st Infantry Division to First Army (assigned): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-05-05 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0675; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 5 May 1945 (first row without it 6 May 45); C0675 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-05-05); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0356` 100th Infantry Division to VI Corps (unspecified_subordination): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-04-24 (inferred_from_next_claim, end_unknown false); found while adding C0688; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 24 April 1945 (first row without it 25 Apr 45); the table lists the entry in 2 runs (1944-11-01 to 1944-11-26, 1945-03-25 to 1945-04-24), the gaps between them are not modeled, as for the other table relations; C0688 is the unit's next claim and does not list it; the end date is the table's, not the day before that claim.
  - `CR0417` 2nd Infantry Division to First Army (assigned): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-05-05 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0667; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 5 May 1945 (first row without it 6 May 45); C0667 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-05-05); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0418` 2nd Infantry Division to V Corps (unspecified_subordination): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-05-05 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0667; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 5 May 1945 (first row without it 6 May 45); the table lists the entry in 2 runs (1944-12-11 to 1945-04-27, 1945-05-01 to 1945-05-05), the gaps between them are not modeled, as for the other table relations; C0667 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-05-05); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0423` 99th Infantry Division to First Army (assigned): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-04-18 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0668; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 18 April 1945 (first row without it 19 Apr 45); C0668 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-04-18); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0438` 69th Infantry Division to V Corps (unspecified_subordination): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-04-27 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0683; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 27 April 1945 (first row without it 28 Apr 45); C0683 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-04-27); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0448` 9th Armored Division to First Army (assigned): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-05-05 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0669; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 5 May 1945 (first row without it 6 May 45); C0669 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-05-05); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.
  - `CR0450` 1st Infantry Division to VII Corps (unspecified_subordination): valid_to was open (end_unknown false); now 1945-04-26 (assignment_table, end_unknown false); found while adding C0675; reason: the CMH ETO-OB table lists it until 26 April 1945 (first row without it 27 Apr 45); C0675 states the end through a link (asserts_end, stated_effective_to 1945-04-26); it is read from the table, not stated by a narrative source.

**18 claims added, 17 new places (486 to 503 places), 1 placeholder(s) promoted to active: 16th Armored Division.** Fifth commit of Batch 5: the third period, 1 to 8 May 1945 (the Army Group G and Twenty-first Army surrenders, Schwerin, Innsbruck and the Brenner, Landeck, the Enns line in Austria, Pilsen on 6 May, the 8 May cut-off). Event-based milestones from The Last Offensive (chapters 18 to 20), the CMH ETO-OB command-post lists, ww2byday listings of corps headquarters and Wikipedia (Plzeň) as the second family for the 0.9 row. **Placeholder promoted:** 16th Armored Division (the 13th Armored, 86th, 97th, 20th Armored and 71st were promoted in the two previous commits). No claim for the 65th Infantry Division in this period: it was already active (Batch 4) and no source read for Linz gives a command-post date. ## batch5-apr-may-1945: claims 15 to 30 April 1945 (C0664 to C0694) (26 October 2026)

**31 claims added, 30 new places (456 to 486 places), 2 placeholder(s) promoted to active: 20th Armored Division, 71st Infantry Division.** Fourth commit of Batch 5: the second period, 15 to 30 April 1945 (the Harz and Leipzig, the Elbe and Mulde line, the first contact with Soviet troops at Strehla on 25 April, Nuremberg, the Danube crossings, Regensburg and Munich with Dachau, the approach to the Alps). ## batch5-apr-may-1945: operations (26 October 2026)

Second commit of Batch 5. **`data/operations.csv`: three rows added (42 to 45 operations); `build/operations.csv`, `build/operation_references.csv` regenerated. No claim, unit, place or relation changed.** The three operations are the ones the claims of the next commits use as `operation_id` (the most specific operation that contains the date; the rows of 1 to 8 May use the campaign `OP-CAMP-CENTRAL-EUROPE`, which has the CMH streamer dates 22 March to 11 May 1945).

- **`OP-RUHR-POCKET`** (Ruhr pocket, battle, parent `OP-CAMP-CENTRAL-EUROPE`, 1945-04-01 to 1945-04-18, `date_basis` `official_history`, R566, R576): the ring closed on 1 April (Last Offensive ch. 16 p. 359-362) and organized resistance ended on 18 April (p. 372; Wikipedia, Ruhr pocket, gives the same end). The two sources differ on when the *reduction* began (Wikipedia 1 April with the Ninth Army; the Green Book, map 5, 4 April); the operation row starts on the date of the closing of the ring, which both give.
- **`OP-HARZ-POCKET`** (Harz pocket, battle, parent `OP-CAMP-CENTRAL-EUROPE`, 1945-04-11 to 1945-04-23, `official_history`, R567): the 1st Division pierced the western fringe on 11 April; the fight ended on 23 April (Last Offensive ch. 17 p. 402-405). The map title ("11-17 April") is shorter than the text; the row follows the text and says so.
- **`OP-NUREMBERG`** (Battle of Nuremberg (1945), battle, parent `OP-CAMP-CENTRAL-EUROPE`, 1945-04-16 to 1945-04-20, `official_history`, R568, R579): assault began 16 April, the city fell on 20 April (Last Offensive ch. 18 p. 424-425; Wikipedia, a five-day battle).
- **Not added:** an operation for the Elbe bridgeheads, Leipzig, the Danube crossings, Munich or the Austrian drive (no source read gives them a name and an end date as an operation; their rows use the campaign). Elbe Day (25 April) is an event, not an operation.
- **Checked:** `validate.py` (OK, 646 claims, 45 operations; the references R566, R567, R568, R576 and R579 are now cited by an operation), `semantic_tests.py`, `build.py`, `build_references.py`.

## batch5-apr-may-1945: references (26 October 2026)

First commit of Batch 5 (1 April to 8 May 1945, approved by Scott on 26 October 2026; branch `batch5-apr-may-1945`, based on `main` 367e9b7). **`data/references_meta.csv`: 16 rows added (R566 to R581; 559 to 575 references in total, the numbering has gaps); `data/references.csv` and the build files regenerated. No claim, relation, place or unit changed.** Added with `scripts/build_references.py --add` and then described by hand (title, citation, type, reliability, used_for); every URL was opened on 26 October 2026 (HTTP 200).

- **The Last Offensive ch. 16 to 20** (R566 to R570; HyperWar mirror of the CMH Green Book): ch. 16 the Ruhr encirclement and its reduction, ch. 17 the Elbe, the Harz and Leipzig, ch. 18 Bavaria, ch. 19 the link-up with the Soviet forces, Austria and the piecemeal surrenders, ch. 20 the last pages (the end of the campaign). Same family and reliability as R515 and R533 to R544.
- **CMH ETO-OB division tables** for the four divisions that had none (R571 13th Armored, R572 16th Armored, R573 20th Armored, R574 71st Infantry; Wayback copies). The 86th and 97th Infantry tables are already R561 and R562.
- **ww2byday.com VI Corps** (R575; command-post list compiled from the CMH order of battle; the other corps and army pages are already R471 to R487).
- **Wikipedia** (second family, one family for all of Wikipedia): Ruhr pocket (R576), Elbe Day (R577), Dachau concentration camp (R578), Battle of Nuremberg (1945) (R579), Plzeň (R580).
- **CMH Combat Chronicle of the 95th Infantry Division** (R581; Wayback copy): the dated list of the division's assignments (it also gives the capture of Dortmund on 13 April); it is the same family as the CMH ETO-OB table.
- **Validator:** the Last Offensive rows R566 to R570 are reported as "not cited by any claim" until the claim commits of this branch; the warning goes away with the first claim that cites each chapter. Checked: `build_references.py --check`, `build.py --check`, `validate.py` (OK, 646 claims).

## corps-followups: CR0447 (95th Infantry Division to XIX Corps) closed (26 October 2026)

Item 3 of the follow-up task (approved by Scott on 26 October 2026). **One relation, its two evidence links and two claim notes changed; no claim, place or unit added or removed (646 claims).**

- **`CR0447`** (US-INF-095D to US-COR-019C, attached, from 1945-03-01, start basis `narrative`, Last Offensive ch. 9 p. 173): `valid_to` old blank, new `1945-03-30`; `valid_to_basis` old `open_at_last_claim`, new `assignment_table`; `end_unknown` old `true`, new `false`. Reason: the CMH ETO-OB assignment table of the 95th Infantry Division (R531) lists XIX Corps in the row of 30 Mar 45 and lists "XXII (Opn)" (XXII Corps, Fifteenth Army) in the row of 31 Mar 45, no longer XIX Corps; the end is therefore the day before the first row that no longer lists it, the same convention as CR0444, CR0445 and CR0446. The CMH Combat Chronicle of the division (Wayback copy of history.army.mil/html/forcestruc/cbtchron/cc/095id.htm, read for this check) has the same list. **The end is table-based, not stated by a narrative source** (no source read says when the attachment ended). The table and the Combat Chronicle list XIX Corps (Ninth Army) again from 2 April 1945, outside the 31 March limit of this pass; no relation is made for that period.
- **Links** (`data/command_relation_claims.csv`, `CR0447` with `C0612` and `C0614`): role old `asserts_start`, new `asserts_start_and_end`; `stated_effective_to` old blank, new `1945-03-30`, `stated_effective_to_basis` old blank, new `assignment_table`; `stated_note` extended with the end and the reason.
- **Claim notes** (`C0612`, `C0614`, `Relationship:` sentence and a `Review` note): old "XIX Corps attached (from 1 Mar 45, narrative)", new "... (from 1 Mar 45, narrative, to 30 Mar 45, CMH table)". On `C0612` the sentence "the disagreement is recorded in command_relation_overlaps.csv" was **wrong and is corrected**: that file never listed the XIII Corps / XIX Corps pair, because CR0446 (operational_control) and CR0447 (attached) are different relationship classes and `scripts/validate.py` rejects an entry for a pair that is not a contradictory overlap (checked). The disagreement (CMH table: XIII Corps operational control from 26 February; Last Offensive: attached to XIX Corps on 1 March) is now stated in the `note` of CR0447, the link notes and the claim notes. Old values and reasons are in each note.
- **Overlaps file:** `data/command_relation_overlaps.csv` unchanged (24 rows). CR0447 and CR0477 (95th to XXII Corps, operational_control from 31 March) are different relationship classes, so they were not a contradictory overlap before and are not one now; the validator accepts both states. Closing CR0447 on 30 March removes the overlap in time.
- **Not changed, flagged:** `CR0477` (95th to XXII Corps, operational_control, from 31 March) stays open (`open_at_last_claim`). The table row of 2 Apr 45 lists XIX Corps again, so the day before (1 April 1945) is the table-based end; that end and the XIX Corps / Ninth Army relations from 2 April belong to the next batch (the pass stops at 31 March) and were not made here.
- **who_controls grid** (before/after on this branch): 5,940 answers, 1 changed (`US-INF-095D`, 5 March 1945: the XIX Corps line "1945-03-01 to open ... no end recorded" becomes "1945-03-01 to 1945-03-30 (assignment_table)"; the answer itself, XIX Corps attached with XIII Corps operational control above, is unchanged). Dates after the end, checked separately: 29 Mar (XIII Corps ends "at an undocumented hour", XIX Corps now certain instead of "XIII or XIX unknown"), 30 Mar (XIX Corps ends that day), 31 Mar to 2 Apr (the stale "XIX Corps or XXII Corps" unknown is gone; XXII Corps operational control is the answer).
- **Derived files:** `build/all_claims.csv` (two notes), `build/command_relations.csv`, `docs/review/README.md` (counts: end basis open_at_last_claim 231 to 230, assignment_table 77 to 78; link roles asserts_start 863 to 861, asserts_start_and_end 103 to 105). `docs/review/sample.md` unchanged. `scripts/semantic_tests.py`: no expectation changed (68 hold).
- **Audit finding explained (`audit_temporal_relationships.py`):** the `overlap` finding for US-INF-095D (XIII Corps operational_control against XIX Corps attached) now reads 1945-03-01 to 1945-03-29 (before: 1 to 5 March, bounded by the last claim that listed the open relation), and its count goes 2 to 4 because it is printed once per claim pair (C0612, C0614); `end_unknown` 60 to 59 (CR0447). This is the documented CMH table / Last Offensive disagreement, not a new one; nothing is resolved. `audit_command_location.py` output unchanged.
- **Checked:** `validate.py` (OK, 135 claim files, 646 claims, 356 units, 14 conflict groups; the three known warnings), `semantic_tests.py`, `build.py --check`, `build_references.py --check`, `review_sample.py --check`, both audits.

## corps-followups: formed_date of XXII and XXIII Corps (26 October 2026)

Item 1 of the follow-up task (approved by Scott on 26 October 2026; branch `corps-followups`, based on `main` after the merge of Batch 4 and the corps task). **`data/units.csv`, two rows; no claim, relation or place changed.**

- **`US-COR-022C` (XXII Corps), `formed_date`:** old `1944-01-09`, new `1944-01-15`. `formed_date_source` unchanged (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XXII_Corps_(United_States), R159). Reason: the column holds the formation or activation date, not the constitution date (`schema/README.md`, `docs/methodology.md`: "formed_date is formation or activation"); 9 January 1944 is the constitution date and the Lineage section of the page, read on 26 October 2026, gives "Activated 15 January 1944 at Fort Campbell, Kentucky". `unit_note` old "Wikipedia infobox start date is marked as a constitution/lineage date; the activation date may differ.", new a note that gives the lineage text, the old and new values and the reason.
- **`US-COR-023C` (XXIII Corps), `formed_date`:** old blank, new `1944-01-15`; `formed_date_source` old blank, new https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XXIII_Corps_(United_States) (R560, already a reference; `build/unit_references.csv` gains one row). Reason: the introduction of the page, read on 26 October 2026, says the corps "was activated on 15 January 1944 at Camp Bowie, Texas". 9 January 1944 is not used: the infobox gives it as the start of the active span, and the History section gives it as the constitution date of the Corps Artillery headquarters battery, so the page does not say outright that the corps itself was constituted that day. `unit_note` was blank, now records this, the old and new values and the reason.
- **Inferred values:** none; both dates are stated by the page.
- **Derived files:** `build/coverage.csv` (the two `formed_date` cells), `docs/coverage.md` (US corps "With formed_date" 5 to 6, all 234 to 235), `build/unit_references.csv`, `data/references.csv` (R560 `units_count` 0 to 1). `scripts/semantic_tests.py`: no expectation changed (68 hold).
- **Checked:** `validate.py` (OK, 135 claim files, 646 claims, 356 units, 14 conflict groups; the three known warnings), `semantic_tests.py`, `build.py --check`, `build_references.py --check`.
